To navigate the feature space, a generator LLM proposes semantic definitions, a separate extractor LLM materializes the features, and a downstream tabular model evaluates their predictive performance. We optimize this search by translating explicit model errors, such as AUC ranking inversions, into natural-language feedback, steering the LLM to resolve specific predictive failures. Evaluated across three public datasets, this error-driven loop accelerates feature discovery by up to $3\times$ compared to unguided search. Empirically, the generated features demonstrate strong multi-view complementarity, strictly outperforming any subset when combined with TF-IDF and dense embeddings. Finally, the framework guarantees instance-level interpretability: the discovered features dominate SHAP importance rankings and provide a fully transparent, semantic audit trail for every prediction.

</details>


### [145] [ExpBoN: Exponential-Noise Best-of-$n$ for Efficient Test-Time LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21899)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExpBoN——一种基于指数噪声 report-noisy-max 机制的软 Best-of-n 采样方法，并集成到引导投机推理框架中得到 ExpGSI，实现高效奖励引导的 LLM 对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-n（BoN）采样是一种简单有效的推理时对齐方法，但硬最大化对奖励与分布偏移之间的权衡控制过于粗糙。Soft BoN 虽提供更平滑的控制并收敛到 KL 正则化奖励最大化的最优分布，但仍有改进空间。本文旨在提出一种新的软 BoN 方法，提供更优的理论收敛性质与计算效率。

Method: 提出 ExpBoN，基于指数噪声的 report-noisy-max 机制。该方法具有精确的有限 n 分解，在总变差、期望奖励及两个方向的 KL 散度上均实现指数级快速收敛。作者对其收敛性和遗憾行为进行了全面的理论分析。进一步将 ExpBoN 集成到引导投机推理（GSI）框架中，得到 ExpGSI，用于高效的奖励引导 LLM 对齐。

Result: ExpGSI 在保持相当精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。在 MATH500、MMLU-STEM、Minerva Math 上，使用 Qwen2.5-Math 和 Qwen3 模型族进行实验：Qwen2.5-Math 在各候选预算下估计计算量减少 14%–39%；Qwen3 在 n=16 时减少最多 45%。

Conclusion: 研究结果为指数噪声 BoN 及高效的推理时 LLM 对齐提供了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: Best-of-$n$ (BoN) sampling is a simple yet effective inference-time alignment method, but hard maximization provides only coarse control over the trade-off between reward and distribution shift. Soft Best-of-$n$ (Verdun et al. 2025) provides smoother control and converges to the optimal distribution associated with KL-regularized reward maximization. In this paper, we introduce ExpBoN, an alternative soft BoN method based on the exponential-noise report-noisy-max mechanism. It admits an exact finite-$n$ decomposition, which yields exponentially fast convergence in total variation, expected reward, and both directions of KL divergence. We provide comprehensive theoretical analyses of its convergence and regret behavior. We further integrate ExpBoN into the guided speculative inference (GSI) framework (Geuter, Mroueh, and AlvarezMelis 2025), resulting in ExpGSI, for efficient reward-guided LLM alignment. ExpGSI yields substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining comparable accuracy. Experiments on MATH500, MMLU-STEM, and Minerva Math with the Qwen2.5-Math and Qwen3 model families show that ExpGSI reduces estimated computation by $14\%$-$39\%$ across candidate budgets for Qwen2.5-Math and by up to $45\%$ at $n=16$ for Qwen3. Overall, our results provide a theoretical and algorithmic foundation for exponential-noise BoN and efficient test-time LLM alignment.

</details>


### [146] [Intervention Granularity Matters: Coherent Treatment Bundles in Counterfactual Simulation with Clinical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21906)
*Fangzhou Wang,Yixuan Yang,Camilla Balzarotti,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究临床世界模型在反事实模拟中的"干预粒度"问题。基于MIMIC-IV的945,707患者小时共现审计显示，临床干预通常以"捆绑包"形式记录（如透析回路各参数从不单独出现）。作者用潜世界模型Clin-JEPA在1,019次有创通气起始点上，对比"单个呼吸机设置编辑"与"完整配置捆绑包编辑"的预测效果，发现捆绑包编辑使预测的下一状态移动更远，且该差异在控制输入变化量后仍存在。结论：干预粒度会实质影响临床世界模型的响应，单组件编辑可能低估治疗敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟需要先定义"什么算一次干预"。但临床干预往往被记录为捆绑包（如透析回路各参数从不单独出现），单独修改某一组件会描述数据中从未出现的情形，因此需要检验干预编辑的粒度是否会影响世界模型的反事实响应。

Method: 1) 对MIMIC-IV中945,707个患者小时做共现审计，确认干预以捆绑形式共现；2) 提出假设：干预编辑粒度会改变世界模型响应；3) 采用Clin-JEPA——以小时级治疗文本为条件的患者轨迹潜世界模型；4) 在1,019个有创通气起始点上，固定患者历史与其他治疗，对比"编辑单个呼吸机设置"与"编辑真实相似轨迹患者记录的完整配置"两种方式。

Result: 完整捆绑包编辑使预测的下一状态移动幅度大于任何单一设置编辑，且该结果在全部五种设置上一致；在控制每种编辑对模型输入改变量的多少之后，差异依然存在。

Conclusion: 干预粒度会实质性影响临床世界模型的响应：单组件编辑可能低估治疗敏感性，而捆绑感知（bundle-aware）的编辑方式可能为反事实治疗模拟提供更有依据的基础。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation with a clinical world model means fixing a patient's history, changing the treatment, and reading off the predicted response. Doing so requires deciding what counts as one intervention. In clinical settings, interventions are documented as bundles: a co-occurrence audit of 945,707 patient-hours from MIMIC-IV shows groups of components, such as every parameter of a dialysis circuit, that never appear apart, so an edit that changes one component on its own describes an hour that never occurs in the data. We hypothesize that the granularity at which an intervention is edited changes how a world model responds, and test this with Clin-JEPA, a latent world model of patient trajectories conditioned on hourly treatment text. At 1,019 documented onsets of invasive ventilation, we keep the patient's history and other treatments fixed and compare editing one ventilator setting with editing the complete configuration recorded for a real patient with the most similar recent trajectory. The complete bundle moves the predicted next state further than any single setting, consistently across all five settings, and the difference remains after accounting for how much each edit changes the model's input. Intervention granularity therefore materially affects the response of a clinical world model: single-component edits may understate treatment sensitivity, and bundle-aware editing may offer a better-supported basis for counterfactual treatment simulation.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim两种运动模仿强化学习流水线，发现两者运动学精度相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更符合实验EMG，更适合肌骨建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动模仿强化学习流水线虽能高保真复现人体运动学，但能否准确捕捉产生运动的神经肌肉行为尚不明确；这对以肌肉激活模式和代谢成本为优化目标的机器人辅助设备设计至关重要。

Method: 采用同一套人体运动捕捉与肌电（EMG）测量数据，对SCONE/HyFyDy（强调详细肌腱建模的生理真实性）和MuJoCo/MyoSim（强调计算效率与可扩展策略学习）两条流水线进行系统性对比。

Result: 两条流水线均能以相对精度产生相似的运动学；但HyFyDy的肌肉激活与实验EMG更吻合，肌肉激活的平均汇总（RMSE, r）值分别为HyFyDy (0.164, 0.4)与MuJoCo (0.344, 0.11)。

Conclusion: HyFyDy更先进的生理真实性使其当前更适合肌骨建模，但两者均需进一步发展，以将生理真实性引入GPU可并行仿真环境，从而推进机器人辅助设备设计。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [148] [Kinks vs. Smoothness: Identifiability of Real Analytic nICA for Laplace-like Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21926)
*Isaac Manring,Kejun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在实解析生成函数且源分布密度函数一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点（如拉普拉斯分布）的条件下，非线性独立成分分析（nICA）可实现精确可辨识，并在归一化流与变分自编码器上通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性独立成分分析的核心挑战是恢复真实的潜在独立因子，而非其被打乱的版本。以往可辨识性结果对生成函数或源分布的限制较强，作者希望放宽条件，使结论适用于更广泛的生成机制与实际训练流程。

Method: 作者利用源分布中的"折点"（kinks）与实解析函数光滑性之间的对比来进行证明。假设生成函数为实解析函数，源概率密度函数的一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点（拉普拉斯分布为典型例子），在此条件下证明可辨识性（在平凡歧义范围内精确恢复）。该框架可用归一化流或变分自编码器（tanh、softplus、GELU等标准激活函数）近似，因而几乎无需改动即可应用于现有训练流程。

Result: 在真实数据与合成数据上，使用归一化流和变分自编码器进行实验，均展示了其可辨识性质。在CelebA数据上，成功恢复了若干可解释的潜在因子，这些因子控制着数据集中独特的属性。

Conclusion: 该工作为实解析生成函数下的非线性独立成分分析提供了可辨识性理论保证，涵盖拉普拉斯等常见源分布，并与主流生成模型训练管线兼容，理论与实验共同表明其方法能恢复有意义的潜在因子。

Abstract: Many machine learning systems try to explain complex data - like images or financial time series - in terms of hidden, independent factors that generated them. Recovering the true underlying factors, rather than some scrambled version of them, is the central challenge of nonlinear Independent Component Analysis (nICA). We prove identifiability (exact recovery) up to trivial ambiguities for real analytic generating functions when source probability density functions have a finite number of discontinuities in the first derivative. The Laplace distribution is the most prominent example satisfying this assumption. Our proof relies on the contrast between kinks in the source distribution and the smoothness of real analytic functions. Real analytic functions comprise a broad class of generating mechanisms, and can be approximated with Normalizing Flows or Variational Autoencoders with standard activation functions (e.g., tanh, softplus, GELU), so our result applies with minimal changes to existing training pipelines. We perform experiments on real and synthetic data with both Normalizing Flows and Variational Auto-Encoders demonstrating their identifiability properties. In experiments on CelebA data we recover several interpretable latent factors controlling unique attributes across the dataset.

</details>


### [149] [Joint Remaining Useful Life Prediction and Capacity Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries Using Partial-Charging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21932)
*Khoa Tran,Ho-Si-Hung Nguyen,Phone Wai Yan Moe,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Thi-Hoang-Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"跨专家"框架，利用部分充电测量数据（无需历史满循环容量）联合预测电池剩余使用寿命（RUL）与容量，通过双专家表征与特征级线性调制实现融合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合预测RUL与容量需要同时表征电池的渐进退化趋势与近期行为，而现有方法往往依赖历史满循环容量测量，实际应用中难以获取，因此需要一种仅用部分充电数据即可同时完成两项任务的方案。

Method: 框架包含两个专家：RUL Expert 以30循环历史中采样的10个循环的10分钟名义片段为输入，采用预训练GRU编码器、2D-CNN与时间GRU；Capacity Expert 以连续10个循环的40分钟名义片段的统计描述符为输入，采用2D-CNN与Transformer。通过特征级线性调制模块，用短期表征对长期表征进行条件化以实现联合预测。训练分三阶段：监督式自编码器预训练、专家独立预训练、冻结专家后的融合训练。

Result: 在两个公开电池老化数据集上，参考配置的RUL平均RMSE分别为143.69与161.10循环，容量误差分别为12.36与7.28mAh。在数据集I上，融合相对任一单专家均降低了两项平均误差。方法在两个数据集上取得所比较方法中最低的RUL RMSE，但若干基线在容量误差上更低。

Conclusion: 结果表明所提框架在RUL预测上具有优势，且RUL与容量精度之间存在权衡关系；融合策略能在部分指标上同时改善两项任务，但容量精度仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Joint remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and capacity estimation require representations of both gradual degradation and recent battery behavior. This paper presents a cross-expert framework using partial-charging measurements without measured historical full-cycle capacity as an input. The RUL Expert encodes nominal 10-min segments from ten cycles sampled within a 30-cycle history using a pretrained gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder, a two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2D-CNN), and a temporal GRU. The Capacity Expert processes statistical descriptors of nominal 40-min segments from ten consecutive cycles using a 2D-CNN and a Transformer. A feature-wise linear modulation module uses the short-term representation to condition the long-term representation for joint prediction. Training comprises supervised autoencoder pretraining, independent expert pretraining, and fusion training with frozen experts. On two public battery-aging datasets, the reference configuration achieves mean RUL root-mean-square errors of 143.69 and 161.10 cycles and capacity errors of 12.36 and 7.28mAh, respectively. On Dataset I, fusion reduces both mean errors relative to either standalone expert. The results demonstrate a trade-off between RUL and capacity accuracy: the proposed method attains the lowest reported RUL RMSE among the compared methods on both datasets, whereas several baselines yield lower capacity errors.

</details>


### [150] [Learning to Move Cities: Deep Meta-Models and Reinforcement Policies for Calibration and Control in Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21945)
*Adewumi Augustine Adepitan,Christopher J. Haruna,Oluwasegun Adegoke,Ayooluwatomiwa Ajiboye,Oluwatobi Oluwasakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个共享潜空间框架，将仿真器校准与强化学习控制通过城市交通动力学的公共学习表示连接起来，在基准路网上将系统总出行时间最多降低51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通网络优化面临高保真仿真器校准和实时运行控制两大挑战，二者通常被割裂处理，且在大规模网络中传统优化方法存在计算瓶颈。因此需要一个统一的表示框架来同时支持校准与控制。

Method: 首先构建组合式MLP自编码器架构，学习连接仿真器输入（OD需求、网络参数）与输出（出行时间、拥堵模式）的低维流形，用于高效贝叶斯优化校准；其次实现带经验回放和目标网络的深度Q学习智能体，通过调度与路径调整优化动态交通分配；并将学习到的潜表示同时用于降维校准和强化学习状态表示。

Result: 在基准网络上的实证评估显示，相比传统降维方法，该方法在固定计算预算下样本效率更高、对观测数据拟合更好；相比基线运营策略，系统总出行时间最多降低51%。

Conclusion: 共享潜空间公式为智能交通系统提供了从仿真器校准到自适应运行控制的统一路径，体现了深度学习方法在城市出行规划与管理、尤其是大规模网络中的变革潜力。

Abstract: Urban transportation networks present complex optimization challenges spanning calibration of high-fidelity simulators and real-time operational control. This paper presents a shared latent-space framework that connects simulator calibration and reinforcement learning control through a common learned representation of urban traffic dynamics. First, we develop a combinatorial MLP-autoencoder architecture that learns low-dimensional manifolds linking simulator inputs (origin-destination demand, network parameters) to outputs (travel times, congestion patterns), enabling efficient Bayesian optimization for calibration. This approach demonstrates superior sample efficiency compared to traditional dimension reduction methods, achieving better fit to observational data within fixed computational budgets. Second, we implement a deep Q-learning agent with experience replay and target networks to optimize dynamic traffic assignment through scheduling and routing adjustments. In empirical evaluations on benchmark networks, our approach reduces system-wide travel times by up to 51% compared to baseline operations. The learned latent representation is not only used to reduce the dimensionality of Bayesian calibration, but is also incorporated into the reinforcement learning state representation, allowing the control policy to operate on compressed and calibrated traffic dynamics. This shared latent-space formulation provides a unified pathway from simulator calibration to adaptive operational control within intelligent transportation systems. Our results highlight the transformative potential of deep learning methods in urban mobility planning and management, particularly for large-scale networks where traditional optimization approaches face computational bottlenecks.

</details>


### [151] [RACER: Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Human-AI Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21953)
*Joshua Strong,Emma Sun,Alexander Capstick,Pramit Saha,Cheng Ouyang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER，一种角色相对的能力估计框架，用于在无专家身份信息下估计未见专家的能力并进行延迟决策，理论证明其具有类别重标记不变性，并在多个基准上取得竞争性或最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种群自适应延迟需利用少量专家行为上下文来估计未见专家能力。已有神经上下文编码器（如L2D-Pop）可能学习与绝对类别坐标绑定的路由捷径；Identity-Free Deferral（IFD）虽消除捷径，但其估计在每个类别内为常数，无法刻画实例级的专家专长。

Method: 提出RACER（Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing），一个角色相对框架：在每个候选类别角色下估计专家在查询上正确的后验预测概率，再与模型后验结合得到贝叶斯相关的专家正确概率。采用非参数与神经核池化估计器，利用候选角色关系、共享聚合与对称摘要，排除绝对类别身份通道。理论方面证明了类别重标记不变性、推导了贝叶斯对齐的延迟代理，并给出将路由遗憾与分类器及能力估计误差关联的插件式遗憾界。

Result: 在受控合成基准上：在隐藏亚型依赖下，RACER能从更多上下文中获益；在CIFAR-100合成实验的独立采样未见专家划分上取得最强总体性能。在放射科与人类-AI胸片基准（VinDr-CXR和CheXpert）上，RACER系列在预算扫描的延迟决策中具有竞争性或最优表现，校准结果因指标和数据集而异。

Conclusion: RACER通过角色相对的能力估计，在无身份信息条件下实现了对未见专家实例级能力的有效估计，具备理论保证与良好的实证表现，是对现有种群自适应延迟方法（L2D-Pop、IFD）的改进。

Abstract: Learning to defer asks a predictive system when to act autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. Population-adaptive deferral extends this problem to unseen experts using a small context set of expert behavior. Neural context encoders such as L2D-Pop can be query-dependent, but may learn routing shortcuts tied to absolute class coordinates. Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) removes such shortcuts through role-indexed classwise competence profiles, but its estimates are constant within each class and cannot capture instance-level expert specialization. We propose RACER---Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing---a role-relative framework for estimating an unseen expert's competence from context. RACER estimates the posterior-predictive probability that the expert is correct on a query under each candidate class role, then combines these estimates with the model posterior to obtain the Bayes-relevant expert-correctness probability. Nonparametric and neural kernel-pooling estimators use candidate-role relations, shared aggregation, and symmetric summaries, excluding absolute class-identity channels. We prove coherent class-relabelling invariance, derive a Bayes-aligned deferral surrogate, and give a plug-in regret bound relating routing regret to classifier and competence-estimation error. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, including a PathMNIST histopathology context-scaling study with simulated experts, RACER benefits from additional context under hidden subtype dependence and gives the strongest aggregate performance on a separately sampled unseen-expert split in the CIFAR-100 synthetic experiments. On the radiologist and human--AI chest-radiography benchmarks (VinDr-CXR and CheXpert), the RACER family is competitive or best in budget-swept deferral, with calibration results varying across metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [152] [Time series generation with spectrally aligned latent flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21989)
*Camilo Carvajal Reyes,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频谱对齐的潜在流（latent-flow）时间序列生成器，通过在潜在空间训练中引入基于傅里叶、小波和签名变换的微调损失，使合成信号在平滑度与频谱内容等方面与真实信号对齐，在真实长程单变量和多变量基准上优于基线及当前最优方法，并兼具信号真实性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在流模型是高效、低成本的时间序列生成方法，但潜在压缩会引入不想要的伪影（如与原始数据集的频谱不匹配），从而限制其作为训练替代品（training surrogate）的可用性。现有方法多依赖逐点重构损失，难以保证合成样本在动力学特性上的适用性。

Method: 提出频谱对齐的潜在流时间序列生成器：对用于流匹配的潜在空间进行训练，使其保留与合成样本适用性相关的动力学性质。具体做法是引入基于规范信号表示（傅里叶变换、小波变换、签名变换）的微调损失。借助这些变换的可解释性，确保合成信号在平滑度、目标频谱内容等相关特征上与真实信号对齐，而非仅依赖逐点重构损失。

Result: 在真实世界的长程单变量与多变量基准数据集上，将所提出的对齐模型与基础潜在流模型及当前最优方法进行比较。定量结果表明，所提方法在反映信号真实性（signal realness）的指标和计算效率上均具优势，同时在局部结构上与训练集保持对齐。

Conclusion: 基于规范信号表示的微调损失可有效克服潜在压缩带来的频谱失配等伪影，使潜在流生成器在保持计算效率的同时提升合成信号的真实性与与训练集的局部结构一致性，验证了频谱对齐策略对时间序列生成的有效性。

Abstract: Latent flow models have proven to be a reliable and cost-effective method for time series generation. However, the latent compression induces unwanted artefacts, such as a spectral mismatch with respect to the underlying dataset, thus hindering their use as training surrogates. In this article, we propose a spectrally-aligned latent-flow time series generator, where the latent space for flow matching is trained to preserve dynamical properties that are relevant for the suitability of synthetic samples. We find that incorporating fine-tuning losses based on canonical signal representations such as the Fourier, wavelet and signature transforms helps overcome these issues. The interpretability of these transformations allows us to ensure that the synthetic signals are aligned with the true ones in terms of relevant features, such as smoothness or targeted spectral content, as opposed to relying on pointwise reconstruction losses only. We compare the proposed aligned models against a base latent-flow model and the state of the art over real-world long-range univariate and multivariate benchmark datasets. Our quantitative results validate the superiority of the proposed method in terms of its performance on metrics reflecting signal realness and computational efficiency, while being aligned to the training set with respect to its local structure.

</details>


### [153] [Assessment of Machine Learning-Based Critical Heat Flux Models in the CTF Subchannel Code for Square Rod Bundle Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21995)
*Aidan Furlong,Vinicius de Melo Monteiro,Robert Salko,Juliana Pacheco Duarte,Xu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在CTF子通道程序中评估基于机器学习的临界热流密度（CHF）模型在EPRI棒束数据库上的表现，发现纯模型与混合残差修正模型（局部/半局部形式）总体优于传统方法，其中局部混合LUT模型表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHF是核热工水力中的关键安全量，直接影响燃料性能与反应堆安全。尽管机器学习方法相比传统经验关联式和查找表显著提高了CHF预测精度，但多数ML模型仅基于管状数据库开发与评估，其在反应堆相关棒束几何中的适用性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在CTF子通道代码中部署基于机器学习的CHF模型，使用EPRI棒束CHF数据库进行评估；考察纯ML模型与混合残差修正模型，并分别采用局部与半局部形式；与传统方法（Bowring关联式、W-3关联式、2006 Groeneveld LUT）进行对比。

Result: 管状数据训练的ML CHF模型总体能较好地迁移到棒束应用，在多数几何和工况下优于传统CHF方法；局部混合LUT模型整体性能最强，半局部纯ML模型也极具竞争力；即使仅用管状数据训练，棒束CHF预测仍可获得显著改进。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在生产级子通道分析环境中对ML-based CHF模型进行大规模方形棒束评估，支持其在反应堆热工水力分析中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: The prediction of critical heat flux (CHF), a key safety-related quantity in nuclear thermal hydraulics, remains an important challenge due to its direct relationship with fuel performance and reactor safety. Recent studies have demonstrated that relative to traditional empirical correlations and lookup tables (LUTs), machine learning (ML) methods can substantially improve CHF prediction accuracy. Most ML-based CHF models, however, have been developed and evaluated using tube databases, leaving their applicability to reactor-relevant rod bundle geometries largely unexplored.
  This study evaluates ML-based CHF models deployed within the CTF subchannel code using the Electric Power Research Institute (EPRI) rod bundle CHF database. Both pure and hybrid residual correction models are considered in local and semilocal formulations. The tube-trained ML CHF models generally transferred favorably to rod bundle applications and outperformed traditional CHF methods across most geometries and operating conditions. The local hybrid LUT model produced the strongest overall performance, and the semilocal pure ML model remained highly competitive. Comparison against the Bowring correlation, W-3 correlation, and 2006 Groeneveld LUT demonstrated that substantial improvements in rod bundle CHF prediction are possible even when models are trained exclusively on tube data. These findings provide one of the first large-scale assessments of ML-based CHF models in square rod bundles within a production-level subchannel analysis environment and support their broader application in reactor thermal hydraulic analysis.

</details>


### [154] [Abstention and Noise Filtering: Two Missing Primitives of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22005)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证并实验证明：注意力门控之所以能提升语言模型预训练，是因为它提供了 softmax 注意力所缺失的两种原语——弃权（abstention）与噪声过滤（noise filtering），且二者的收益随模型规模呈现相反趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究观察到门控注意力能改善语言模型预训练，但对原因解释不一。作者希望厘清门控机制究竟带来了什么，从而解释不同规模下收益来源的差异。

Method: 在 10M 到 350M 参数的匹配模型上进行实验：通过 softmax 中可学习的 per-head sink logit 实现弃权，通过在每个 value 上加门控实现噪声过滤；并注入受控干扰以验证门控的去干扰作用。

Result: 三项发现：（1）弃权带来的收益（相对基线的验证损失下降）随规模增大而减小，噪声过滤的收益随规模增大而增大；10M 时弃权几乎贡献全部增益，350M 时过滤贡献大部分。（2）每个规模下同时具备两种原语的模型表现最好。（3）受控干扰实验证实门控确实消除干扰，并揭示两种门控形式各有其固有盲区。

Conclusion: 门控的价值来自弃权与噪声过滤两种互补原语；同时引入二者几乎不增加参数，且与 key-value cache 兼容，是一种高效且可扩展的注意力改进方案。

Abstract: Gating the value pathway of attention reportedly improves language model pretraining, and prior studies disagree on why. We argue and provide experimental evidence that such gates supply two different things that softmax attention lacks: abstention and noise filtering. The first is abstention, which allows an attention head to output nothing, bypassing the requirement that attention weights must sum to one. The second is noise filtering, which allows the value pathway of an attention head to suppress interference from superposed features in the residual stream. In our experiments in matched models from 10M to 350M parameters, we supply abstention through a learned per-head sink logit in the softmax and noise filtering through a gate on each value. We report three empirical findings. First, the benefit of abstention, measured as the reduction in validation loss relative to a matched baseline, declines as models grow, whereas the benefit of noise filtering increases with scale. In particular, abstention accounts for nearly all of the gain from gating at 10M and filtering for most of it at 350M. Second, the best model at every scale is the one with both primitives built in. Third, injecting controlled interference into the values a head reads confirms that the gate removes such interference, and reveals that each of the two gate forms we study has a characteristic blind spot. Supplying both primitives adds negligible parameters and remains compatible with the key-value cache.

</details>


### [155] [COMPLEX: A Closed-Form Certified Embedding of Multiparameter Persistence Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22012)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COMPLEX：一种无需训练、闭式的多参数持久性嵌入方法，首次为多参数特征映射给出双边失真界，使"忠实性"可度量，并在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多参数持久性向量化方法只具备单侧Lipschitz上界，缺少下界（下侧gauge），因此无法保证特征忠实性，也无法构建逐预测的认证保证。本文旨在补齐这一缺失的一侧。

Method: COMPLEX：对多参数模沿固定近对角网进行切片，用经过认证的PLACE/PALACE地标映射嵌入每个切片条形码，再拼接。在可检验的"见证切片一致性"条件下，单个切片即可携带闭式下界gauge，结合标准上界得到首个双边失真界。

Result: 下界在实测距离的小因子内是紧的，但操作上是局部的：同一特征上RBF-SVM达91%，1-NN仅78%，说明逐预测的局部认证因结构性原因失效。无需学习嵌入与留出校准（仅用交叉验证SVM头），COMPLEX在Orbit5k（91.95%）与Orbit100k（92.98%）达到SOTA，并超过GRIL在全部四个共享分子基准上的表现。

Conclusion: COMPLEX提供了首个多参数特征映射的双边失真界，使忠实性可度量。闭式选择（地标半径、保证书核、双滤流集合）可进一步提升精度，而基于梯度形状的自适应则无增益。

Abstract: Every multiparameter persistence vectorization we know of carries a one-sided Lipschitz upper bound and nothing below it: without a lower gauge there is no sense in which the features are faithful, and no per-prediction guarantee can be built on them. This paper supplies the missing side. COMPLEX is a closed-form, training-free embedding of multiparameter modules -- slice the module along a fixed near-diagonal net, embed each slice barcode by the certified PLACE/PALACE landmark map, concatenate. Under a checkable witnessing-slice coherence condition, holding on 100% of audited pairs on Orbit5k, a single slice carries a closed-form lower gauge: separated modules stay separated in the embedding. With the standard upper bound this gives, to our knowledge, the first two-sided distortion bound for a multiparameter feature map, making faithfulness measurable. Measuring it, we find the floor tight within a small factor of realized distances yet operationally local: an RBF-SVM reaches 91% where 1-NN reaches 78% on the same features. Local per-prediction certification therefore fails for a structural reason common to every landmark embedding whose lower gauge is witnessed by one coordinate. With no learned embedding and no held-out calibration -- only a cross-validated SVM head -- COMPLEX sets the state of the art on both Orbit benchmarks (91.95% on Orbit5k, 92.98% on Orbit100k), level with or above Euler-characteristic surfaces and above transformers and graphcode. On graphs it exceeds GRIL on all four shared molecular benchmarks with one fixed configuration, including the only multiparameter method to clear COX2's majority baseline by more than three points. Closed-form selection -- of the landmark radius, the kernel (certificate-preserving), and the bifiltration set -- buys further accuracy; gradient-shaped adaptation buys none.

</details>


### [156] [$λ$-Controlled GRPO: Turning Flow-Matching Ratio Instability into a Budgeted Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22041)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Meeshawn Marathe,Ramit Pahwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 λ-Controlled GRPO，将 flow-matching 强化学习训练中的多步去噪不稳定统一归因于可估计的"路径方差"，并据此校准重要性比值与梯度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-GRPO 将去噪采样器视为随机策略进行奖励优化，但训练不稳定且表现出多步去噪特有的症状：重要性比值随去噪步骤漂移、低于 1、离散度增大、裁剪率不同、后期可用样本减少。既有工作把这些视为彼此独立的失败模式，逐一用手工调参的稳定器修补。

Method: 作者指出这些症状来源于单一的逐步量——路径方差，它由采样器的高斯转移核精确决定，并可在训练中低成本估计。据此提出 λ-Controlled GRPO：基于预测规律而非噪声经验统计来校准重要性比值行为，并按预测代价在去噪步骤间分配梯度资源；控制更新的两个尺度由标准策略选择固定，不再作为自由调参参数。

Result: 在文本到图像模型、两种奖励设定（OCR 评分渲染困难目标文本、偏好模型评分的人类偏好匹配）下，λ-Controlled GRPO 在文本准确率和偏好奖励上均优于最强的经验稳定器，并将后期步骤路径方差控制在预算内，而基线在此处系统性超支。

Conclusion: Flow-GRPO 的更新可由其自身的转移规律进行校准，而不必在出现不稳定后再行修补；将不稳定视为可测量、可预算的资源是更有效的框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is increasingly used to align image generators with reward signals, and Flow-GRPO recently extended this paradigm to flow-matching models by treating the denoising sampler as a stochastic policy that can be optimized from reward feedback. Training in this setting is unstable in a way specific to multi-step denoising: the policy update changes systematically across denoising steps, with importance ratios drifting below one, becoming increasingly dispersed, clipping at different rates, and leaving fewer usable samples late in training. Prior work treats these effects as separate failure modes and addresses each with a hand-tuned stabilizer. We show instead that they arise from a single per-step quantity, which we call path variance. This quantity is determined exactly by the sampler's Gaussian transition kernel and can be estimated cheaply during training. This reframes instability as a resource that can be measured and budgeted rather than a collection of symptoms to repair. Our method, $λ$-Controlled GRPO, calibrates importance-ratio behavior from this predicted law rather than from noisy empirical statistics, and allocates gradient effort across denoising steps according to their predicted cost. The two scales governing the update are fixed by standard policy choices rather than introduced as free tuning parameters. On a text-to-image model under two reward settings, rendering difficult target text scored by optical character recognition and matching human preferences scored by a preference model, $λ$-Controlled GRPO improves both text accuracy and preference reward over the strongest empirical stabilizer. It also keeps late-step path variance within its intended budget, precisely where the baseline systematically overshoots. The result is a Flow-GRPO update calibrated by its own transition law rather than stabilized after instability appears.

</details>


### [157] [Available Guardrails: Certifying Selective Prediction across ML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22048)
*Parivesh Priye,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling,Michael Chaykowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究选择性预测器（安全门）在有限校准数据下能否对每个报告单元颁发达到目标精度的认证，提出以精确二项反演衡量"可用性"，并将报告分区选择建模为动态规划，揭示安全性、粒度与覆盖流量之间的权衡前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景要求选择性预测器对每个报告单元（如工具、策略标签、患者亚组）在目标精度上获得认证。核心难点不在于已颁发证书是否有效，而在于有限校准数据能否产出证书：当门更安全或粒度更细时，某些单元可能缺乏足够证据完成认证。作者希望使这种"可用性"可计算并可为部署规划。

Method: 通过经典精确二项分布反演使可用性可计算；在固定组序下将报告分区选择形式化为动态规划，刻画安全性、粒度与所服务流量之间的权衡；在一个规划划分上构造候选分区、在另一个划分上选择；并采用在报告单元间重新分配族错误率预算这一保有效性的手段。

Result: 所得前沿揭示出有限样本估计几乎抹去的大规模人口机会：真实信息规划者相比支持度平衡可获得 0.157 的平均覆盖增益，而朴素估计器仅恢复 0.005。跨划分的候选构造与选择恢复了部分差距，平均覆盖提升 0.060，且在三个意图路由数据集、两种架构的 60 个模型效应中有 59 个方向一致。重新分配族错误预算可带来额外覆盖增益。该前沿在 LLM 工具调用、内容审核、病变分类与推荐中重复出现，仅预测器上限不同。

Conclusion: 认证可用性是一种可规划部署的资源，它决定了安全门何时能被认证、以何种粒度认证以及覆盖多少流量。有限数据下的恢复是核心挑战。

Abstract: A selective predictor acts as a safety gate: it returns an output only when the prediction appears sufficiently trustworthy. Deployments increasingly require this reliability to be certified at a target precision for every reporting unit of interest, such as a tool, policy label, or patient subgroup. The main difficulty is often not whether a granted certificate is valid, but whether finite calibration data can produce one at all. As the gate becomes safer or more fine-grained, some units may receive too little evidence to certify. We make this notion of availability computable through classical exact-binomial inversion and formulate reporting-partition selection, under a fixed group order, as a dynamic program that exposes the trade-off among safety, granularity, and served traffic. The resulting frontier reveals a large population opportunity that finite-sample estimation nearly erases: a truth-informed planner gains $0.157$ mean coverage over support balancing, whereas a naive estimator recovers only $0.005$, making recovery from finite data the central challenge. Constructing candidate partitions on one planning split and selecting among them on another recovers part of this gap, improving mean coverage over support balancing by $0.060$, with the direction reproduced in $59$ of $60$ model effects across three intent-routing datasets and two architectures. A complementary validity-preserving lever, reallocating the familywise error budget across reporting units, recovers additional coverage both with population quantities and noisy estimates. The same frontier recurs, with predictor-specific ceilings, across LLM tool-calling, content moderation, lesion classification, and recommendation. Certified availability is therefore a plannable deployment resource that determines when a safety gate can be certified, at what granularity, and over how much traffic.

</details>


### [158] [Particle Competition and Cooperation for Robust Graph Convolutional Network Learning Under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22053)
*Fabricio Breve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PCC+GCN，将粒子竞争与合作（PCC）作为图标签精修预处理阶段，在 GCN 训练前识别并保留、删除或重分配可疑标签节点，从而提升 GCN 在标签噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图卷积网络（GCN）对标签噪声高度敏感，被污染的监督信号会沿图结构传播，进而损害学习到的节点表示。因此需要一种有效的预处理策略来缓解噪声标签的影响。

Method: 提出 PCC+GCN 混合框架：先用 PCC 通过粒子支配动态识别可疑标注节点，决定其标签是保留、删除还是重新分配；PCC 使用的图可引入基于特征的 k 近邻边增强，而 GCN 仍在原始图结构与节点特征上训练。在 NoisyGL 基准的十个图数据集上，针对 Uniform、Pair、Random 以及实例相关标签噪声进行评估，并在 Cora、CiteSeer、PubMed 上进行超参数分析。

Result: 在常规噪声下，PCC+GCN 取得最高总体平均准确率与最佳平均排名，相较基线 GCN 在干净设置及所有噪声场景下平均提升 1.67 个百分点；在实例相关噪声下与最优鲁棒方法保持竞争力，且执行时间显著更低，在十个数据集中的八个上为最快的鲁棒方法。

Conclusion: 基于 PCC 的标签精修为提升 GCN 在噪声监督下的鲁棒性提供了一种有效且计算高效的预处理策略。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are highly sensitive to label noise, since corrupted supervision can propagate through the graph and degrade learned node representations. This work proposes PCC+GCN, a hybrid framework that uses Particle Competition and Cooperation (PCC) as a graph-based label-refinement stage before GCN training. PCC identifies suspicious labeled nodes through particle domination dynamics and determines whether their labels should be preserved, removed, or reassigned before GCN training. The framework also allows the graph used by PCC to be augmented with feature-based $k$-nearest-neighbor edges, while the GCN itself is trained on the original graph structure and node features. The proposed method was evaluated on ten graph datasets from the NoisyGL benchmark under conventional Uniform, Pair, and Random label noise, as well as under instance-dependent label noise. A detailed hyperparameter analysis was also conducted on Cora, CiteSeer, and PubMed. Under conventional noise, PCC+GCN achieved the highest overall average accuracy and the best average rank among the evaluated methods, with an average gain of $1.67$ percentage points over the baseline GCN across the clean setting and all noisy scenarios. Under instance-dependent noise, PCC+GCN remained competitive with the best-performing robust methods while requiring substantially lower execution time, being the fastest robust method on eight of the ten datasets. The results indicate that PCC-based label refinement provides an effective and computationally efficient preprocessing strategy for improving GCN robustness under noisy supervision.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking World Models for Continual Learning on Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22055)
*Haoyu Zhou,Joe Watson,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准，通过设计组合任务课程并沿动作与感知两个轴进行分解，隔离评估世界模型对先验经验的知识复用能力，发现模块化世界模型在复用与遗忘之间的权衡优于常规方法，但仍未彻底解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型理想的属性是能够跨任务持续学习、适应新环境而不遗忘已有知识。由于物理世界动态常由重复出现的机制组成，复用先验经验对高效适应新环境至关重要。然而，现有世界模型的适应能力度量混杂了两种能力：学习未见任务的速度与容量，以及已获取知识的复用，因为新任务往往同时包含新内容与重复内容。为将知识复用从先验经验中隔离出来，需要专门设计的评测基准。

Method: 作者提出一个面向机器人操作世界模型的组合式持续学习基准。具体做法是为每个任务课程设计组合任务，将序列中已见任务的若干方面进行组合；并进一步沿动作与感知两个轴对组合进行分解，以理解不同输入模态如何成为知识复用的瓶颈。实验在典型持续学习方法下评估了当前最先进的世界模型，同时对比了一个动力学主干包含显式可复用组件的模块化世界模型。

Result: 结果表明，模块化设计在复用与遗忘之间的平衡优于常规持续学习方法，但没有一种方法能完全解决该问题，为构建"复用而不遗忘"的持续世界模型留下了明确的研究空间。

Conclusion: 该工作通过组合式持续学习基准隔离并评估世界模型的知识复用能力，揭示了现有方法在复用与遗忘权衡上的不足，指出模块化是更优但非充分的方案，为持续世界模型研究提供了方向与评测工具。

Abstract: A desirable property of a world model is the ability to learn continually across tasks, adapting to new environments without forgetting what the agent has already learnt. In particular, the ability to retain and reuse knowledge obtained from prior experiences underpins an agent's ability to efficiently adapt to novel environments, as the dynamics of the physical world can often be described in recurring mechanisms. However, the world model's measure of adaptation entangles two abilities: the speed and capacity to learn unseen tasks, and the reuse of knowledge already acquired, since incoming tasks carry novel content alongside what recurs. In order to isolate knowledge reuse from prior experiences, we propose a compositional continual learning benchmark for world models in robot manipulation. Specifically, we design each task curriculum with compositional tasks that combine aspects of the tasks seen in the sequence. We further factorise this composition along the axes of action and perception to better understand how different input modalities bottleneck knowledge reuse. We evaluate state-of-the-art world models under canonical continual learning methods, alongside a modular world model whose dynamics backbone contains explicitly reusable components. Results show that modularity balances reuse against forgetting better than conventional methods, but none solve the problem fully, leaving clear room for continual world models built to reuse without forgetting. More details are available on our project website: https://object814.github.io/Compositional-Continual-Learning/.

</details>


### [160] [BrainWideBench: Benchmarking large-scale pretraining and across-animal transfer in multi-region neural recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22064)
*Alexandre Andre,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Vinam Arora,Keshav Balaji,Divyansha Lachi,Nanda H. Krishna,Jingyun Xiao,Yizi Zhang,Ximeng Mao,Wenrui Ma,Han Yu,International Brain Laboratory,Daniel Birman,Niccolò Bonacchi,Gaelle A. Chapuis,Joana A. Catarino,Felicia Davatolhagh,Mayo Faulkner,Laura Freitas-Silva,Fei Hu,Julia M. Huntenburg,Anup Khanal,Inês Laranjeira,Petrina Lau,Guido T. Meijer,Nathaniel J. Miska,Jean-Paul Noel,Alejandro Pan-Vazquez,Georg Raiser,Cyrille Rossant,Karolina Z. Socha,Anne E. Urai,Miles J. Wells,Steven J. West,Olivier Winter,Blake Richards,Guillaume Lajoie,Cole Hurwitz,Mehdi Azabou,Matthew R. Whiteway,Liam Paninski,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 BrainWideBench 基准，基于 IBL 全脑图谱数据（139只小鼠、276个脑区）评估跨动物神经表征迁移，发现现有预训练方法迁移能力异质，尚无方法在三类任务上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经记录技术已能跨多动物、多脑区采集数据，理论上可利用这种规模学习通用的神经表征并迁移到多样下游任务。但该目标进展受限，原因在于评估协议碎片化，且研究多局限于单一任务领域，缺乏统一、可复现的评测框架。

Method: 构建 BrainWideBench 基准，数据来自 International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map，涵盖139只小鼠执行感知引导决策任务时的276个脑区神经与行为记录。基准设计三个互补任务套件：行为解码、掩码或未来神经活动预测、生物解剖组织结构的恢复。在该基准上系统评估多种预训练方法在不同迁移设置下的表现，包括下游目标微调与对未见动物的零样本泛化。

Result: 预训练相较匹配的单会话基线能提升性能；但现有方法迁移能力存在异质性，增益强烈依赖预训练目标与下游任务的对齐程度。没有任何单一方法在三个套件上均表现优异，且多数方法仅针对其中一部分任务设计。

Conclusion: 学习能够同时跨行为、神经动力学与解剖结构泛化的表征仍是未解难题。BrainWideBench 提供统一可复现的评测套件，为衡量小鼠大脑通用模型的进展建立框架。

Abstract: Advances in large-scale neural recording have made it possible to collect data across many animals and distributed brain regions, raising the question of whether this scale can be exploited to learn general-purpose neural representations transferable across diverse downstream tasks. Yet, progress toward this goal has been limited by fragmented evaluation protocols and a narrow focus on individual task domains. Here, we present BrainWideBench, a benchmark for evaluating across-animal transfer on multi-region neural recordings, built on the International Brain Laboratory Brainwide Map dataset of neural and behavioral recordings spanning 276 brain regions from 139 mice performing a sensory-guided decision-making task. The benchmark is organized around three complementary task suites that evaluate whether learned representations support downstream decoding of behavior, can predict masked or future neural activity, and can recover biologically meaningful anatomical organization. With this benchmark, we systematically evaluate pretraining methods across transfer settings, including finetuning on downstream objectives and zero-shot generalization to unseen animals. Our results confirm pretraining improves performance over matched single-session baselines, but we show current methods exhibit heterogeneity in transfer capabilities: gains depend strongly on the alignment between pretraining objectives and downstream tasks. No single approach performs uniformly well across all three suites, and most methods are designed to only address a subset of them. Together, these findings suggest that learning representations that jointly generalize across behavior, dynamics, and anatomy remains an open challenge. By providing a unified and reproducible evaluation suite, BrainWideBench establishes a framework for measuring progress toward general-purpose models of the mouse brain.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WM-VS目标中心、进度对齐的世界模型框架，解决闭环视觉伺服中"预测-控制失配"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服需要预测动作能否减小任务误差，而不仅是动作是否合理，这一差距被称为"预测-控制失配"。

Method: 用离线目标区域DINOv2对应关系定义平移、尺度、面内旋转的带符号四维伺服坐标；阶段1将动作条件潜变量转移与该坐标对齐；阶段2冻结世界模型，训练反应式联合速度策略，结合动作模仿、后果监督与短想象 rollout；部署仅用RGB、反应式、无在线轨迹优化。

Result: 真实7-DoF眼在手系统上，30/30试验角点RMSE不超过初值10%，25/30（83.33%）在最后有效帧保持该标准；去除未来误差对齐后降至26.67%；学习进度信号与外部AprilTag误差一致（平均Spearman rho=0.8778）；两个未见3D目标无重训下平移误差降低86.48%、90.27%，旋转误差降低70.01%、65.70%。

Conclusion: 结果将进度对齐的动作后果与重复闭环校正及迁移能力联系起来，代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [162] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑双模态空中-地面机器人AeRove，利用桨叶保护罩作为滚轮沿管道滚动，配合弹簧双稳态机构实现快速模式切换，大幅降低功耗并提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距管道检测对标准无人机具有挑战性：悬停功耗高、气流敏感，且螺旋桨气流会干扰气体传感。

Method: AeRove将螺旋桨保护罩用作滚轮沿管道滚动，采用弹簧加载双稳态机构在200 ms内完成地面与飞行模式切换，且两种状态均无需持续致动器供电维持；收敛式保护罩几何提升推力效率；飞行仅用于越障，地面滚动用于连续行进；通过自主试验在51 cm直径钢管上测试，并单独开展CO2传感实验对比栖停与空中条件及不同进气布局。

Result: 电流消耗相比持续飞行降低14倍（0.7 A vs. 10 A），估计行驶距离从144 m提升至2057 m；自主试验实现直线与弯曲管道导航、越障及模拟标记泄漏检测；栖停检测的浓度响应显著大于悬停；飞行时底部螺旋桨进气布局比延伸探头响应更快更强。

Conclusion: AeRove通过双模态滚动-飞行设计与螺旋桨气流利用，有效解决近距管道检测的功耗与气流干扰问题，代码与设计以CC-BY许可开源。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


### [163] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出"摇一摇即学习"(shake-to-learn)的机械探测策略：用折纸启发式软体机械臂作为物理储备池计算机，通过固定抖动激励物体并记录余振响应，可推断视觉无法感知的隐藏物理属性(如质心)，并完成质心方向推断、距离推断及重抓取引导三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中许多关键物理属性(如密封物体的质心、内部结构)无法通过视觉等常规传感获知，往往需要提举、摇晃等动态扰动才能显现。作者希望将这种交互引发的动态响应转化为一种新的机器人感知与学习模态，从而推断常规传感无法获取的物体物理信息，并服务于下游操作任务。

Method: 以折纸启发式软体机械臂构建物理储备池计算机(reservoir computing)。抓取物体后，在臂基部施加固定抖动激励，通过相机追踪或嵌入式传感器记录随之产生的余振(ringdown)响应。由于输入在各次试验中保持不变，隐藏的物体属性(如质心位置)通过其对"机器人-物体"耦合系统动力学的影响被编码，再用轻量级线性读出器解码这些动力学，并进一步提出余振响应的动态摘要表示以提升预测精度。

Result: 该软体臂储备池系统完成了三项难度递增的任务：(1)推断物体隐藏质心的方向；(2)推断质心距抓取点的距离；(3)利用推断出的质心信息引导后续重抓取。所提出的动态摘要表示进一步提高了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"摇一摇即学习"式机械探测作为机器人系统的一种可行策略：系统可将短暂的物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，用于下游操作任务。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [164] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForeTac-VLA，一种基于触觉预测的视觉-语言-动作融合模型，通过预测未来触觉状态指导动作生成，在四项真实接触密集操作任务中平均成功率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖视觉感知，在接触密集环境中鲁棒性不足，因为关键物理交互状态可能无法从视觉中观测。已有的触觉增强VLA方法虽利用观测到的触觉反馈提升物理感知，但大多是被动反应式，未能显式建模接触将如何演变。

Method: ForeTac-VLA将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；采用基于Transformer的预测模块预测多步未来触觉状态，从而联合推理已观测与预期的接触；将融合的多模态表示与预测的未来触觉状态输入VLA主干以条件化动作生成。训练时采用从真值到预测的课程学习策略，以稳定早期不可靠的预测。

Result: 在四项真实接触密集操作任务上，ForeTac-VLA平均成功率达95%，比微调后的VLA模型高出36.25个百分点，比最先进的触觉增强VLA基线高出22个百分点以上；在低照度和视觉杂乱条件下仍保持强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测未来触觉状态并联合观测与预期接触进行推理，ForeTac-VLA显著提升了接触密集操作任务的鲁棒性与成功率，优于现有VLA及触觉增强方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention. Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on https://foretac-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [165] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIVOT 将 VLM 语义推理与几何规划结合，用物理实测校准地形可通过性评分，并通过两级导航架构在几何规划失败时触发语义重规划，使越野自主性从 59.6% 提升至 97.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野移动机器人需要在非结构化、几何复杂环境中安全可靠导航。传统基于几何的地形评估计算快，但在非结构化环境中往往过于保守，容易导致路径找不到、需人工干预。

Method: 提出 PIVOT 系统：以视觉语言模型（VLM）进行语义推理，补充几何规划；量化 VLM 预测的通行能量代价、机器人振动与车轮打滑同真实测量的相关性，并据此加权构建统一可通过性评分；设计两级导航架构，默认使用几何规划，仅在其找不到路径时启用语义重规划。

Result: 在混合地形约 6.4 km 路线上进行五次重复闭环试验，相比纯几何导航：整体自主性由 59.6% 提升至 97.0%，人工干预由 11 次降至 3 次，平均干预间隔距离（MDBI）由 69.2 m 提升至 412.9 m。

Conclusion: 经物理校准的 VLM 地形评估能显著超越纯几何方法的自主导航能力边界，同时保留高效几何规划作为常规模式，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [166] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转式FMCW雷达的多普勒测速能否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能。作者构建了自动标注流程，在Boreas数据集上训练和评估检测器与跟踪器，结果表明多普勒去畸变可提升检测精度，多普勒速度先验可显著提升跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转式FMCW雷达在自动驾驶感知中因抗恶劣天气和360°视野而受关注，且能提供逐方位多普勒速度。但尚不清楚这些多普勒测量是否能改善旋转雷达的车辆检测与跟踪性能。此外，带真值动态目标标注的多普勒旋转雷达数据缺失，是研究的主要障碍。

Method: 针对检测，估计自车运动并用其消除雷达图像的多普勒距离畸变，再输入网络。针对跟踪，提出新的逐车辆速度估计方法，并将其作为跟踪器运动模型的先验。为解决数据缺失，构建自动标注流程：使用一组微调的现成激光雷达检测器对Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km进行标注，再将检测结果迁移到雷达，使用超过250 km的车辆密集序列作为真值训练数据。

Result: 训练并评估两个最先进检测器后，多普勒去畸变使平均精度（mAP）最多提升2.37个点。多普勒速度先验相比零速度初始化基线，使多目标跟踪精度（MOTA）提升13.68个点，并达到使用真值速度先验所得MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒测速能有效提升旋转式雷达的车辆检测与跟踪性能，去畸变与速度先验分别带来检测和跟踪的显著增益，接近使用真值速度的上限，验证了多普勒信息在自动驾驶感知中的价值。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [167] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT——一个ROS集成的机载感知与控制框架，用于对来袭无人机进行实时空中防御，通过机载视觉检测、目标关联、轨迹滤波与控制生成，实现无需外部基础设施的"守门"式拦截跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的无人机快速增多，周界防御亟需鲁棒且抗干扰的反无人机系统；现有方案多依赖外部传感基础设施或地面站计算，难以满足机载实时性需求。

Method: SCOUT部署于防御无人机机载端，感知流程由TensorRT加速的无人机检测、基于ByteTrack的目标关联以及轻量级轨迹保持状态机构成；状态机可剔除目标突跳并在检测短暂退化时保持目标连续性，从而抑制误检或目标切换引起的不稳定控制响应；最终通过平面"守门"拦截策略生成控制指令。

Result: 真实飞行实验显示，SCOUT在实时机载检测速率下，92.2%的帧保持有效目标检测；在防御边界附近维持干扰配置，验证了视觉跟踪与闭环控制用于无人机周界防御的可行性。

Conclusion: SCOUT证明了仅靠机载视觉感知与闭环控制即可实现实时的无人机周界防御跟踪，为抗干扰反无人机系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. https://figshare.com/s/497befc033091fc0b84f

</details>


### [168] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将临时障碍下的机器人导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程，提出基于POMCP的信念空间规划器SPARROW，结合在线生存模型与学习价值准则，在仿真和实物实验中显著降低到达目标时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在规划路线上可能遇到临时障碍，需要决定等待障碍清除、重新规划路径，还是先获取更多障碍信息。这种序贯决策问题具有部分可观测性和不确定性，现有方法难以同时处理潜在障碍类别、清除时间以及替代路线上的新障碍。

Method: 将图导航中的临时障碍问题形式化为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程；提出SPARROW，一种基于部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）的信念空间规划器。SPARROW在遍历、观测和有限时长等待动作上进行搜索，并维护关于潜在障碍类别与清除时间的粒子信念。采用类别条件生存模型，通过清除观测和机器人在清除前改道产生的右删失遭遇进行在线学习。使用生成模型模拟每个动作展开时的障碍到达与清除，从而考虑替代路线上的阻塞。此外，引入学习价值准则，在收集带标签生存数据的即时成本与未来导航遗憾的预期降低之间进行权衡。

Result: 在两个仿真图和多种障碍类别设置中，SPARROW相比近期基于生存模型的方法OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低12-26%。在实体移动机器人上，SPARROW相比OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低20.5%，并能随环境条件变化选择性地观测、等待和改道。

Conclusion: SPARROW通过信念空间规划、在线生存学习和学习价值准则，有效解决了临时障碍下的序贯导航决策问题，在仿真与实物实验中均优于现有方法，并展现出适应环境变化的能力。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [169] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究证明，仅使用低于1万美元的消费级传感器（立体相机、MEMS IMU、深度传感器）与开源AUV，即可在完全无约束的6自由度水下环境中实现可用的视觉-惯性SLAM导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV通常依赖昂贵传感器套件（如DVL和USBL），成本高昂限制了先进海洋机器人从专业实验室向更广泛的研究与爱好者应用推广。本文旨在验证低成本平台能否实现稳健高质量的水下导航。

Method: 采用价格相近的开源AUV，评估立体相机、MEMS惯性测量单元和深度传感器在无系缆6自由度水下环境中的表现；分析现成SLAM软件包的有效性，并提出传感器融合优化方案，以缓解水下视觉与物理挑战。

Result: 结果表明，在无工业级硬件支持的情况下，低成本的视觉-惯性SLAM方案可以实现可用的导航效果，为高要求实时海洋任务提供了性能基准。

Conclusion: 可用的SLAM解决方案能够向大众普及，无需承担工业级硬件的经济负担，从而推动海洋机器人技术的广泛应用。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [170] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Feedback 提出一种双时间尺度架构，将低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合，把扩散去噪的最后一步作为轻量反馈接口，在执行前用最新观测校正动作。在静态 LIBERO 任务上匹配 GR00T，在动态仿真任务上平均成功率从 27.5% 提升至 85.0%，真实机器人任务从 51% 提升至 73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型结合预训练视觉语言模型的语义知识与扩散动作生成策略，在机器人操作中表现良好。但扩散生成的动作块在推理后通常以开环方式执行，当物体移动、接触变化或场景演化时，响应能力受限。

Method: 提出 VLA-Feedback 双时间尺度架构：低频扩散规划 + 高频视觉反馈。不预先完整去噪动作块，而是保留最后一去噪步骤作为轻量反馈接口，使每个动作在执行前可用最新观测校正。既保留扩散规划器的表达能力，又无需重跑完整视觉语言扩散模型即可实时纠正动作。

Result: 在静态 LIBERO 任务上匹配 GR00T；动态仿真任务平均成功率从 27.5% 提升至 85.0%；真实机器人任务平均成功率从 51% 提升至 73%。

Conclusion: VLA-Feedback 通过将扩散去噪的最后一步作为反馈接口，实现了高效实时的动作校正，在保持静态任务性能的同时显著提升动态与真实场景下的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: https://vla-feedback.github.io.

</details>


### [171] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了刚体动力学一阶导数的算法，使其适用于约束嵌入建模的闭链运动系统，去除了关节速度效应局部构型不变的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效导数方法假设关节速度效应局部构型不变，无法直接应用于约束嵌入的闭链模型等更一般的关节类型，限制了对包含局部闭链的驱动子机构进行精确动力学计算。

Method: 去除局部构型不变假设，推导出可适配更一般关节类型的动力学导数算法，将原有刚体动力学一阶导数算法推广到约束嵌入的闭链运动系统。

Result: 对比了传统销关节机器人模型与更完整、能捕捉局部闭链的驱动模型；结果表明推广引入的额外项在仅建模驱动运动学时代价很低，但当驱动链中包含额外刚体（如电机转子）或考虑非局部闭环时，计算成本会更高。

Conclusion: 该工作使约束嵌入驱动子机构能进行更精确的动力学计算，可应用于模型预测控制和可微仿真。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [172] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究部分探索地图下的单快照射频发射源定位，比较数字孪生方法与MAPLE-RF两种输出位置后验的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在SLAM探索中往往只有部分地图，而传统RF定位需要完整环境模型来预测障碍物的遮挡与反射，因此需要研究部分已知地图下的定位方法。

Method: 方法一将数字孪生法扩展到部分地图，把未探索空间视为自由空间，并对混合地图覆盖进行训练；方法二MAPLE-RF将估计的到达路径角和信噪比编码为网格通道，与地图已知度、占据情况和视线可见性对齐，用U-Net一次前向为所有候选位置打分，无需推理时模拟传播。

Result: 室内环境射线追踪仿真表明，混合地图覆盖训练对两种方法都至关重要。数字孪生法在多数单快照指标上更准确；MAPLE-RF精度接近，且查询成本不依赖传播模型，比全网格通用射线追踪查询低两个数量级以上。两者均优于高斯和高斯混合基线；在自估计引导的探索路径上，融合的MAPLE-RF后验在源附近放置的概率更高。

Conclusion: 部分探索地图下的RF发射源定位可行，混合覆盖训练是关键；MAPLE-RF以极低查询成本获得接近数字孪生法的精度，适合机器人探索场景。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [173] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA是一个自动化的real-to-sim-to-real框架，能从单张RGB图像生成可部署的灵巧操作策略，实现零样本真机部署，并通过仿真-真实协同训练将成功率从29.2%提升至61.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采集真实世界灵巧操作数据成本高、耗时长；高保真物理仿真器虽能实现可扩展的数据合成与策略学习，但手动构建可直接部署的数字孪生仍劳动密集，且视觉、几何与动力学残差阻碍了可靠的sim-to-real迁移。

Method: DEXTERA包含四个统一阶段：(1) 单图像场景分解为静态高斯背景与可交互刚体/铰接资产，并由VLM推断物理参数；(2) 度量级场景全局对齐、物体规范化与形态均衡的机器人标定；(3) 可扩展的仿真任务原语构建、VR遥操作与以物体为中心的运动轨迹合成；(4) 支持模仿学习与强化学习的共享多模态策略接口。

Result: 在13个任务-具身组合、2个灵巧机器人平台、6种策略架构上评估。DEXTERA在视觉保真度与3D几何重建上优于生成式基线；跨域轨迹重放验证了强物理交互一致性；仅仿真训练的策略可实现可行的零样本真机部署；仿真-真实协同训练将平均物理策略成功率从29.2%提升至61.9%。

Conclusion: DEXTERA通过自动化的real-to-sim-to-real流程，有效缩小了sim-to-real差距，在多种任务、机器人与策略架构上验证了其泛化性与实用性，为灵巧操作的规模化策略学习与部署提供了新方案。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [174] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将轨迹跟踪、推力分配与单推进器力/速率约束统一在一个二次规划中求解的水面无人艇控制架构，并通过外部故障检测器的二值标志实现故障重构，已在苏黎世湖和威尼斯展会的现场表演中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编舞式水上表演要求小型自主水面艇在推进器力与速率限制下精确跟踪路径，且需在推进器故障后仍能维持跟踪；传统级联架构在故障后性能退化明显，因此需要一种能统一处理约束与故障重构的控制方法。

Method: 将轨迹跟踪、推力分配以及每个推进器的力与速率限制统一建模为关于各推进器指令的单个二次规划；故障重构通过来自外部检测器的每个推进器一个二值标志进入优化问题。

Result: 现场试验中，10分钟定点保持的均方根位置误差为1.6 cm，以0.6 m/s跟踪10 m方形的误差为4.3 cm；0.5 m/s时失去前推进器误差增至11.6 cm，失去右舷推进器增至11.5 cm；同时失去两个推进器时以0.4 m/s跟踪方形，均方根误差为1.25 m。与自研级联基线相比，统一形式跟踪标称方形的精度相当（几厘米级），但定点保持更紧，且推进器力约为一半；故障后两者分化，统一控制器保持在参考路径24 cm以内，而级联方法偏离路径。

Conclusion: 统一二次规划控制架构能够在正常与故障工况下实现高精度路径跟踪，并在推进器故障后显著优于级联基线，已成功部署于真实水上表演场景。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [175] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文关注植物阴影模拟，构建了涵盖四种作物的植物生长与阴影数据集，并提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，支持下游农业机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长与农业生产是国家可持续发展和人类生计的基础，前沿AI推动了科学农业发展。阴影是影响植物生长的关键因素，但植物阴影模拟本身具有重要性和内在复杂性，该领域仍需推进以产生更广泛的社会效益。

Method: 论文的主要方法包含两方面贡献：(1) 构建综合的植物生长与阴影数据集，覆盖大豆、番茄、糖甜菜和草莓四种植物，采用俯视视角并辅以沿圆形轨迹运动的光源，在多个生长阶段产生动态阴影，涵盖多样的观测复杂性；(2) 提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，实现未见植物上的真实感阴影生成，模型引入时间条件以实现不同时间阶段的灵活阴影模拟。

Result: 通过定量与定性评估对模型性能进行了检验，模型能够为未见植物生成真实感阴影，并支持感知、光照控制和视角规划等下游机器人任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为植物感知的阴影建模提供了基础性研究，对更广泛的农业与机器人应用具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [176] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将A3C强化学习算法与PID控制器结合，用于四旋翼姿态与轨迹跟踪，通过神经网络动态优化PID参数，性能优于A2C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼具有非线性特性和对扰动敏感的特点，传统PID控制器不够精细，难以应对这些复杂性，因此需要更具适应性的控制方法。

Method: 提出集成A3C算法与PID控制器的控制系统：利用并行智能体通过神经网络动态优化PID参数，并引入系统辨识模块预测系统状态以支持最优控制策略，与标准A2C模型进行对比。

Result: 仿真结果显示两种方法都能准确跟踪；但A3C控制器在损失函数上收敛更好，奖励曲线和损失曲线表明其参数优化效果更优。

Conclusion: A3C方法有效结合强化学习与传统控制，提升了四旋翼控制性能与适应性。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型共轴无人机，通过二自由度摆式机构实现主动推力矢量，以较低机械复杂度解决横滚/俯仰欠驱动问题，并通过LQR控制、EKF状态估计与高保真仿真验证了方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轴旋翼无人机因能效高、体积小而受到关注，但在横滚和俯仰控制上存在固有欠驱动问题。传统斜盘等方案虽能克服该限制，却带来更高的机械复杂度。因此需要一种机械结构更简单、仍能实现主动推力矢量控制的替代方案。

Method: 设计一种配备二自由度摆式机构的共轴无人机以实现主动推力矢量控制；建立完整的拉格朗日动力学模型，不忽略机身、舵机臂和电机组件等各部分的惯性贡献；在悬停平衡点附近线性化后设计LQR控制器；考虑执行器动力学和传感器噪声进行高保真仿真；采用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）融合GPS、气压计和IMU进行高精度状态估计。

Result: 高保真仿真结合执行器动力学与传感器噪声验证了所提架构；EKF能够高精度融合GPS、气压计与IMU估计；结果表明该方法在节能、快速共轴无人机方面具有潜力与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了二自由度摆式推力矢量共轴无人机方案的潜力与可靠性，为节能且高机动的共轴UAV提供了一种机械复杂度较低的替代路径。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium.
