High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [178] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究机器人点球系统中策略信息与身体执行能力的耦合，提出动力学诱导承诺映射（DIC-Map）来估计延续能力并定位承诺时刻，分析约化零和博弈及估计器对学习的影响，实验定位承诺约在接触前0.29秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人博弈中的学习不仅受策略信息约束，还受身体能否执行动作的限制，作者希望研究这种策略与身体执行能力的耦合。

Method: 构建分层人形-四足点球系统：博弈层自博弈策略指挥固定足球全身控制器（S-WBC），人形射门技能由自采集动捕数据初始化，四足扑救技能用强化学习获得；引入DIC-Map估计延续能力、识别首次持续失去终端替代的时刻，并检验剩余交互是否可约化为零和博弈；对对称终端替代给出最优策略集中度的闭式界，并分析经由估计器作用时的一阶学习通道。

Result: 实验将承诺定位在接触前约0.29秒，仅改变球速即可改变延迟覆盖；在四种响应者策略下，替换估计器将扑救率从0.240提升至0.472，而通过等待获得相当读数准确率提升仅达0.246；因可用覆盖项为观测代理，采用后验分析进行均衡比较。

Conclusion: 身体动力学约束会通过承诺时刻和估计器中介的学习通道显著影响机器人博弈的策略学习与表现，估计器质量比单纯等待带来的读数准确率提升对扑救性能更关键。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: https://chris-ruizegeng.github.io/penaltykick/

</details>


### [179] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [180] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V. R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFactory：仅需单次视频扫描、无需物理引擎的高保真数据引擎，通过3DGS重建场景并进行运动学规划与渲染，批量生成视觉逼真的示范数据，用于训练视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练视觉运动策略需要大量与目标环境高度匹配的示范数据，而重新采集成本高昂。现有示范合成方法存在人工投入大、视觉保真度有限或严重依赖物理模拟器等局限，因此需要一种低人力、高保真且不依赖物理引擎的示范生成方案。

Method: 1) 以单次视频扫描为唯一人类输入，将场景重建为可编辑的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）副本；2) 从场景可支持的对象组合任务中采样任务；3) 在几何重建上纯运动学地规划抓取与轨迹，渲染与目标环境视觉匹配的逼真示范；4) 仅在抓取形成阶段通过预训练于交互数据集的接触模型引入接触力相关的物理动力学。

Result: 在两个实验环境中验证端到端“扫描到部署”流程：用于保证可复现性的模拟场景，以及配备实体UR10e机器人的真实工作空间。仅使用合成示范训练的标准扩散策略，成功率分别达到95.1%和84.2%。

Conclusion: GaussianFactory表明，仅凭单次视频扫描即可无需物理引擎地批量生成高保真示范，且这些合成数据足以训练出在模拟与真实环境中均具有高成功率的扩散策略，为降低视觉运动策略训练的数据采集成本提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [181] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对暗光环境下机器人在低功耗机载平台上实时单目定位的难题，提出SYS管道：以低光前馈点图前端加显式匹配门控为核心，实现在极低信噪比下避免输出无信息轨迹，并给出Jetson AGX Orin上的嵌入式执行路径优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在极暗环境（单像素信号接近传感器噪声）下仅凭单个RGB相机、在功耗受限的机载计算机上实时定位。低光重建、实时单目SLAM等各单项约束已有成熟方案，但三者交集处尚无可用系统：离线低光重建虽能恢复-4 dB以下结构却无法实时，而DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等可机载的实时系统在低信噪比下退化甚至失效。

Method: 作者先量化现有方法的失败模式：在最暗的九个暗度等级上，DROID-SLAM在全部九个场景返回全长但无信息（不含相机运动信息）的轨迹，VGGT-SLAM与CUT3R在八个、pi^3在七个、DPV-SLAM在四个上如此。随后提出SYS：基于低光前馈点图前端并带显式匹配门的单目管道。此外给出面向Jetson AGX Orin的嵌入式执行路径：地图、关键帧与后端在384像素、跟踪在256像素运行，并对逐帧位姿求解做两处修正。评估采用标定且可比特精确复现的噪声阶梯、重标注的真实暗光曝光数据，以及来自Boston Dynamics Spot机器人的新暗室视频阶梯。

Result: SYS返回三条被跟踪轨迹、无任何无信息轨迹，在其跟踪范围内误差最低（为无信息上限的24-47%，最强基线为56-73%），覆盖率最窄但可靠。真实机器人视频中86.5%的帧全黑时，作者各配置均在明亮起始段后停止，而DROID-SLAM与DPV-SLAM对全部1178帧都输出了位姿。嵌入式优化为可复现的帕累托改进：一场景下吞吐1.28倍、误差0.964倍，另一场景1.42倍、误差0.68倍，峰值GPU内存减少47%，每个位姿能耗降低29%。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了暗光、实时、机载受限单目定位三者的交集空白，通过匹配门控设计避免在极低信噪比下产生误导性位姿输出，并在嵌入式平台上以更低内存与能耗实现误差与吞吐的双重改进。相较之下，主流实时系统（DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM）在完全黑暗帧上仍持续输出位姿，说明其失败是"静默"的，SYS的保守覆盖策略更具实际可靠性。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [182] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaPusher：针对未见物体的非抓取操作，用元学习动力学模型+自适应运动动力学规划器耦合，实现执行中快速适应与长时程重规划，无需独立数据采集阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未见物体的操作受隐藏物理属性（摩擦、质量分布）影响，仅凭感知无法推断；跨物体经验给出的初始估计不确定，且sim-to-real迁移会进一步退化。交互中适应虽能改善估计，但更新模型会让原规划轨迹失效，因此需要同时兼顾快速动力学适应与能容纳模型演化的规划策略。

Method: 提出MetaPusher框架：1）元学习的动力学模型，在任务执行过程中从交互中快速适应；2）自适应运动动力学规划器，通过复用并精炼已有搜索树来更新长时程规划。两者耦合，使操作与适应无需单独的数据采集阶段。

Result: 在仿真和sim-to-real场景下对未见物体进行评估，与微调、主动学习方法、MPPI控制以及强化学习策略对比，MetaPusher取得更低的预测误差，任务成功率最高提升20%。

Conclusion: 将元学习快速适应与树复用式自适应规划结合，可在无先前物体专项交互条件下有效完成未见物体的非抓取操作，并优于微调、主动学习、MPPI与RL基线。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [183] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，用于多机器人-人协作场景中兼顾决策可解释性与物理安全约束，通过SAIL将CBF约束内化到策略中，并用TALO对齐独立actor更新，实现高成功率与低碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方人机协作要求机器人决策可解释、可审计，同时执行动作需满足物理交互中的安全约束。将可解释决策树策略与CBF滤波结合虽有前景，但在多智能体强化学习中存在两个学习不匹配：安全投影会改变实际作用于环境的动作；耦合的proposal图会使独立优化的actor更新与团队级更新不一致。

Method: 提出安全对齐梯度强化SAGE。其SAIL模块使用可微的有限惩罚proposal映射，同时保留精确CBF二次规划用于执行，保持约束范数敏感性以内化反复激活的安全约束；TALO构建团队感知的更新参考，并用Lyapunov半空间校正来规范独立actor更新。

Result: 双足机器人加人类伙伴的物理实验验证了部署可行性。九个仿真场景中，SAGE成功率达71.0%，每千步碰撞0.5步。消融显示，直接CBF滤波使碰撞频率降低98.5%，但成功率从67.3%降至59.3%。SAIL使proposal违规降低48.8%，proposal-execution校正降低85.2%；TALO使更新一致性差距降低50.8%。

Conclusion: SAGE通过安全对齐梯度方法有效缓解了CBF滤波与多智能体独立更新带来的双重不匹配，在保持安全性的同时显著提升任务成功率，并具备真实人机协作部署能力。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates. Physical experiments with two humanoid robots and a human partner demonstrate deployment feasibility. Across nine simulation scenarios, SAGE achieves a 71.0% success rate with 0.5 collision steps per thousand environment steps. Ablations show that direct CBF filtering reduces collision frequency by 98.5% but decreases success from 67.3% to 59.3%. SAIL reduces proposal violation by 48.8% and proposal-execution correction by 85.2%, while TALO reduces the update-consistency gap by 50.8%.

</details>


### [184] [Diverse and Adaptable Arm Coordination for Octopus-Crawling via Diffusion-Based Uncertainty-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21138)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Heng-Sheng Chang,Kimia Kazemi,Prashant Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散的不确定性感知优化算法（DUO），为模拟的肌肉驱动CyberOctopus学习无示范爬行控制器，首次将扩散控制用于接触丰富的软体多臂机器人，实现多样化协调模式的发现与适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼爬行启发了利用冗余性的软体机器人，但如何发现并组织多样化协调模式以实现适应仍是难题。

Method: 提出DUO算法，在共享控制分布中嵌入多种运动行为；采用对称结构化策略表示，将径向等价控制器折叠到标准方向扇区；使用在线黑盒优化策略发现并保留多样协调模式；并采用控制编辑技术适应新执行器约束。

Result: 模拟章鱼能够在动态物理约束下导航，表明学习到的协调多样性天然促进鲁棒适应；控制编辑无需重训练即可适应新执行器约束。

Conclusion: 学习到的协调多样性使运动冗余成为软体多臂机器人适应的实用资源。

Abstract: Octopus crawling motivates soft robots that exploit redundancy, yet discovering and organizing diverse coordination modes for adaptation remains challenging. To address this, we introduce a Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization (DUO) algorithm that learns demonstration-free crawling controllers for a simulated, muscle-actuated CyberOctopus. This work represents the first application of diffusion-based control to soft multi-arm robots in contact-rich simulations. By embedding a variety of locomotion behaviors within a shared control distribution, this approach enables the simulated octopus to navigate dynamic physical constraints, demonstrating that learned coordination diversity inherently facilitates robust adaptation. The main contributions include: (i) a symmetry-structured policy representation that folds radially equivalent controllers into a canonical directional sector, (ii) an online black-box optimization strategy, the DUO algorithm, that discovers and retains diverse coordination modes, and (iii) a control editing technique that adapts existing controllers to novel actuator constraints without retraining. These results show how learned coordination diversity makes motor abundance a practical resource for adaptation in soft multi-arm robots.

</details>


### [185] [Same World, Different Knowledge: When Isolated Audits Misjudge World-Model Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21155)
*Rui Min,Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Sofiane Lachab,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向世界模型的"信息接口审计"方法，区分保真度缺口与可用性缺口，并通过固定权重干预测量预测误差、输入依赖与闭环收益，在四旋翼MPC中验证修复方案优劣取决于部署时的信息路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一输入故障下表现良好的修复方案，当多个模块共享故障信息时可能反而更差；因此需要一种方法评估不同修复方案在实际部署信息路径下的效果。

Method: 提出信息接口审计，区分保真度缺口（精确输入变为估计）与可用性缺口（输入缺失）；采用固定权重干预，测量预测误差、输入依赖性和配对闭环收益，并纳入重建引入的依赖。在仿真四旋翼模型预测控制中，设置耦合、反号的10%质量/推力标定误差进行测试。

Result: 耦合的反号10%质量/推力标定误差使物理锚定模型成功率从69%降至8%，不确定性训练恢复至65%；风重建恢复了控制收益但继承了标定依赖；正标定偏移下仅重建腐化偏好不确定性训练的重建，而共享腐化偏好基线；加速度诊断揭示了重建偏差与标称模型误差间的补偿，并在扰动观测器中同样观察到。

Conclusion: 修复方案的选择取决于部署中使用的信息路径，而非仅由孤立故障下的表现决定。

Abstract: A repair favored under an isolated input fault can be inferior when deployed modules share the faulty information. We introduce an information-interface
  audit for world models, distinguishing fidelity gaps, where exact inputs become estimates, from availability gaps, where inputs are missing. Fixed-weight
  interventions measure prediction error, input dependence, and paired closed-loop benefit, including dependencies introduced by reconstruction. In
  simulated quadrotor model predictive control, coupled, opposite-sign 10% mass/thrust calibration errors reduce a physics-anchored model's success from 69%
  to 8%; uncertainty training restores 65%. Wind reconstruction recovers control benefit but inherits calibration dependence. For a positive calibration
  offset, reconstruction-only corruption favors uncertainty-trained reconstruction, whereas shared corruption favors the baseline. Acceleration diagnostics
  reveal compensation between reconstruction bias and nominal-model error, also observed with a disturbance observer. Repair selection therefore depends on
  the information paths used in deployment.

</details>


### [186] [MA-LIPP: Cooperative Multi-Agent Load-Aware Informative Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21167)
*Hojune Kim,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体负载感知信息路径规划问题（MA-LIPP），通过异构机器人团队的非同步"dead drop"交接实现采样与运输分工，给出精确MIQP模型与可扩展的Pairwise LNS启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机器人负载感知信息路径规划（LIPP）中采样与运输刚性耦合，机器人须携带所有样本并频繁返回基地，严重限制空间覆盖范围。异构多机器人团队可通过分工（高精度采样器采样、高容量载体运输）突破该瓶颈，但引入了何时、何地、交接何物、交接给谁的复杂协调难题。

Method: 将MA-LIPP形式化为精确的混合整数二次规划（MIQP），并提出可扩展的Pairwise大邻域搜索（LNS）启发式算法。核心机制是允许机器人通过非同步"dead drop"（一处机器人放置样本、另一处稍后取回）进行协作，无需同步会合。

Result: 启发式算法在95.5%的认证案例中达到精确最优解；在最多12个机器人的大规模实例上，相对顺序基线将加权后验方差降低16.1%–19.8%。

Conclusion: MA-LIPP为协作式物理采样任务提供了稳健框架，通过非同步交接解耦采样与运输，在保持近优性能的同时具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Field robotics missions often require physical samples to be returned to laboratories for analysis, making path planning inherently load-aware and order-dependent as accumulated samples increase payload and traversal energy costs. In single-robot Load-Aware Informative Path Planning (LIPP), this rigidly couples sensing with hauling: a solitary robot must transport every collected sample, forcing frequent depot returns that severely restrict its spatial coverage. Heterogeneous multi-robot teams can overcome this bottleneck by dividing labor---enabling high-precision samplers to collect while high-capacity carriers handle transport. However, this introduces a complex coordination challenge regarding when, where, what, and to whom handoffs should occur on top of the LIPP problem. To address this tightly coupled problem, we introduce Multi-Agent LIPP (MA-LIPP), which enables teams to cooperate through asynchronous "dead drops," allowing one robot to deposit samples for another to retrieve later without requiring synchronous rendezvous. We formulate MA-LIPP as an exact Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) alongside a scalable Pairwise Large-Neighborhood Search (LNS) heuristic for complex real-world applications. The heuristic matches exact optima in $95.5\%$ of certified cases and reduces weighted posterior variance by $16.1$--$19.8\%$ relative to a sequential baseline on larger instances of up to 12 robots, providing a robust framework for cooperative physical-sampling missions.

</details>


### [187] [OpenRoIS: A Community-Driven Open-Source Middleware Implementing the Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework for Physical Robots and Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21178)
*Sebastian Carrera Villalobos,Christopher Nolan Arellano,Arne Hitzmann,Edilson Morais Brito,Akira Utsumi,Yukiko Horikawa,Takahiro Miyashita,Lotfi El Hafi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍 OpenRoIS——一个社区驱动的开源中间件，作为 Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework 2.0 标准的具体实现，旨在将平台无关的人机交互标准从规范推向工程实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互服务应用通常依赖某一平台的硬件专用接口，更换硬件就需重写应用。OMG 标准化的 RoIS Framework 2.0 通过平台无关模型和硬件无关的符号消息来缓解这种碎片化，但仅有规范并不能提供实际采用所需的持续维护的实现、SDK 和适配器。

Method: OpenRoIS 采取范式中立、开放开发的实现路线，提供递归引擎架构（单一引擎类同时实现主与子 HRI Engine 角色）、与五个外部 RoIS 接口相区分的内部五方法组件契约、将接口映射到基于 WebSocket 的 JSON-RPC 2.0、单一事实来源的类型流水线（生成三种一致的语言栈）、支持 Web 与 Unity 的 TypeScript 和 C# 客户端 SDK，以及支持 ROS 2 的 Python 适配器 SDK。

Result: 通过统一的 RoIS 接口，Service Application 可以跨越互联网寻址物理机器人和虚拟智能体。所有源代码、接口类型和文档均以 Apache-2.0 许可发布，并在 https://openrois.org/ 开放开发。

Conclusion: OpenRoIS 通过开放、社区驱动、范式中立的开源中间件实现，为 RoIS Framework 2.0 提供从规范到实践的落地路径，促进人机交互应用在不同硬件平台与虚拟智能体之间的可移植性。

Abstract: Service applications for human-robot interaction are commonly written against the hardware-specific interfaces of one platform, so a change of hardware forces a rewrite of the application. The Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework 2.0, standardized by the Object Management Group (OMG), addresses this fragmentation by defining a platform-independent model in which Service Applications interact with Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) Engines through standardized interfaces and hardware-independent symbolic messages. A specification alone, however, does not provide the maintained implementation, Software Development Kits (SDKs), and adapters needed for practical adoption. This paper presents OpenRoIS, a community-driven open-source middleware providing a concrete implementation of the RoIS Framework 2.0. It takes the position that an openly developed, paradigm-neutral implementation is what carries the standard from specification to practice. OpenRoIS contributes a recursive engine architecture in which a single engine class realizes the main and sub HRI Engine roles, an internal five-method component contract distinct from the five external RoIS interfaces, a mapping of those interfaces onto JSON-RPC 2.0 over WebSocket, a single-source-of-truth type pipeline that generates three consistent language stacks, TypeScript and C# client SDKs that include web and Unity support, and a Python adapter SDK that includes ROS 2 support. Through the common RoIS interfaces, a Service Application can address physical robots and virtual agents over the internet. All source code, interface types, and documentation are released under the Apache-2.0 license and openly developed at https://openrois.org/.

</details>


### [188] [When to Waddle: A Comparative Study of Bipedal Torso-Stabilization on Low-Friction Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21185)
*Naomi Oke,Ben Gu,George Ortiz,Stacy Ashlyn,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受企鹅摇摆步态启发，研究在低摩擦表面上通过躯干侧向运动和质心（COM）位置来改善双足行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低摩擦表面限制蹬步时的接触力，给双足运动带来挑战；企鹅摇摆步态可能提供一种适应低摩擦的策略。

Method: 使用五驱动器双足机器人，对比直立步态与"躯干越过支撑腿"的企鹅式策略，在仿真（MuJoCo，摩擦系数μ=0.1/0.3/0.5/0.7）和硬件（μ=0.12、0.45）中测试不同COM位置。

Result: 仿真中低摩擦下躯干越支撑腿运动成功率更高、前进速度更快，高速出现在高COM配置；硬件在μ=0.12时前进速度提高、运输成本降低，高COM最优；μ=0.45时趋势反转，低COM更快更节能，步态策略对速度影响小但仍改变侧向足部运动。

Conclusion: 躯干侧向运动与COM位置的效果取决于可用摩擦，企鹅式躯干运动可调节前进速度、能耗和打滑相关足部运动。

Abstract: Low-friction surfaces challenge bipedal locomotion by limiting the contact forces available during stepping. Inspired by penguin waddling, we investigate how lateral torso motion and center of mass (COM) placement affect locomotion as surface friction changes. Using a five-actuator biped, we compare an upright-gait strategy with a penguin-inspired torso-over-stance-leg strategy across multiple COM placements in simulation and hardware. In the 3-D simulator MuJoCo, we sweep through sinusoidal leg and hip actuation parameters across four friction coefficients mu = 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7. In simulation, torso-over-stance-leg motion produces more successful controllers and higher forward speeds at low friction, with the highest speed occurring for the high-COM configuration. Hardware experiments show the same low-friction speed trend: at mu=0.12, torso-over-stance-leg motion increases forward speed and reduces cost of transport at both tested COM ratios, and the higher COM also improves both measures. The high-COM penguin configuration is the fastest and most energy efficient while maintaining low sideways foot motion. At mu=0.45, the COM trend reverses: the lower-COM configurations are faster and more energy efficient, while gait strategy has little effect on forward speed but still changes sideways foot motion. These results show that the effects of lateral torso motion and COM placement depend on the available friction, and that forward speed, energy use, and slip-related foot motion can be modulated with a penguin-inspired torso motion on hardware.

</details>


### [189] [Robust Structureless Monocular Visual Inertial Initialization Exploiting Line Features and Vanishing Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21186)
*Junwan Choi,Woongrae Jo,Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIM-init：一种无结构单目VIO初始化方法，利用2D线特征几何约束，在退化运动下实现稳健准确的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO的准确初始化对可靠性至关重要，但在退化运动下常处于病态条件。现有方法要么需要严格的激励运动以保证可观性，要么依赖计算昂贵的3D结构重建，限制了高效实用的部署。

Method: 提出SLIM-init，一种无结构单目VIO初始化器，直接利用跟踪的2D线特征几何约束，无需显式3D地标重建。具体地：利用线导出的消失点（VP）作为平移不变的方向线索，在低视差或平移主导等退化场景下提供稳健的纯旋转约束；引入线对极残差约束平移，并采用线法向投影残差改善线性对齐的条件数。

Result: 在公开基准和具挑战性的自定义退化运动序列上的大量实验表明，相比最先进的初始化方法，该方法在精度和鲁棒性上均有提升。源码已开源。

Conclusion: SLIM-init通过直接利用2D线特征的几何约束，避免了对激励运动和昂贵3D重建的依赖，为退化运动下的单目VIO提供了高效且稳健的初始化方案。

Abstract: Accurate initialization is essential for reliable visual-inertial odometry (VIO), but it is often ill-conditioned under degenerate motions. Existing methods typically require restrictive excitation motions to ensure sufficient observability or rely on computationally expensive 3D structure reconstruction, limiting efficient and practical deployment. To address these limitations, we propose SLIM-init, a structureless monocular VIO initializer that directly exploits geometric constraints from tracked 2D line features without explicit 3D landmark reconstruction. Specifically, SLIM-init leverages line-derived vanishing points (VPs) as translation-invariant orientation cues to provide robust rotation-only constraints under degenerate scenarios such as low-parallax or translation-dominant motions. It further incorporates a line epipolar residual to constrain translation and a line-normal projection residual to improve the conditioning of linear alignment, enhancing the accuracy and robustness of initial state estimation. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and challenging custom degenerate-motion sequences demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over state-of-the-art initialization methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/cjunwan/SLIM-init.

</details>


### [190] [Stochastic Neural Signed Swept Volume for Real-time Chance-Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21211)
*Qingyi Chen,Kevin Zhang,Lucas Chen,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出学习扫掠体系有向距离函数的概率场，以量化认知不确定性并整合感知噪声，最终用于机会约束轨迹优化框架，在仿真和真实硬件的高维操作任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞运动规划需要可靠的碰撞模型，并对连续轨迹上的状态进行验证。为便于处理，多数规划器仅在连续轨迹的离散状态上、针对单一确定性环境模型检查碰撞，从而在安全性与计算效率之间形成权衡。现有的连续碰撞检查方法（扫掠体积近似）计算代价高或过于保守；数据驱动方法虽可学习扫掠体积，但神经模型易受近似误差影响，通常只能作为下游碰撞检测器的粗过滤器。

Method: 将扫掠体积的有向距离函数学习为一种概率场，从而支持认知不确定性的量化、感知噪声的纳入，并最终集成到机会约束的轨迹优化框架中。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上，针对具有显著传感器噪声的高维操作难题进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: 通过学习扫掠体积概率化有向距离函数，可在量化不确定性与融合感知噪声的前提下支持机会约束轨迹优化，适用于高维、含噪的真实操作场景。

Abstract: Collision-free motion planning requires reliable collision models from sensed environments and validation of states along a continuous trajectory. To make this tractable, most planners check for collision at discrete states along continuous trajectories against a single determinized model of the environment, introducing a trade-off between safety and computational efficiency. While continuous collision checking approaches that approximate the swept volume of the robot exist, they are computationally expensive or overly conservative. Data-driven approaches can learn the swept volume; however, these neural models are susceptible to approximation errors and are therefore often limited to serving as coarse filters for downstream collision checkers. In this work, we propose to learn a signed distance function of the swept volume as a probabilistic field, enabling quantification of epistemic uncertainty, incorporation of perception noise, and eventual integration into a chance-constrained trajectory optimization framework. We demonstrate our approach on challenging high-dimensional manipulation problems with significant sensor noise, both in simulation and on real hardware.

</details>


### [191] [Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21212)
*Beiming Li,Jaime Romero,Jonathan Diller,Vijay Kumar,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VNT-PA，一种以位姿索引的深度关键帧集合为上下文的 Transformer 导航规划器，注意力依赖位姿差异而非时间顺序，在 HM3D 点目标导航中达到 93.3% 成功率与 90.4% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习式导航策略把观测当作按时间排序的历史，并用位置编码把观测绑定到被看到的时间点上，导致难以复用此前在同一环境中的遍历经验；而能够复用经验的系统通常需要显式构建地图或拓扑图并据此规划。本文希望探索位姿标记的经验本身能否直接充当学习规划器的环境表示。

Method: 提出 VNT-PA（Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention）：其上下文是由相机位姿索引的深度关键帧集合，以相机位姿作为位置编码，使注意力取决于关键帧之间的位姿差异而非时间顺序。训练上通过模仿在真值场景网格上运行的最短路径规划器，仅用当前位姿与目标位置查询空间上下文来预测动作。

Result: 在 HM3D 验证场景的点目标导航任务中，VNT-PA 达到 93.3% 成功率和 90.4% SPL，在导航性能与训练效率上均优于将相同上下文编码为时间序列或把位姿作为输入特征的基线；由于空间上下文是位姿索引的集合，测试时可融合不同轨迹的帧；在定位噪声下其性能退化也比在显式地图上规划的传统基线更平缓。

Conclusion: 位姿标记的经验可以直接作为学习规划器的环境表示；让注意力依赖位姿差异而非时间顺序，能够加快训练并改善长时程导航表现。

Abstract: Learned navigation policies typically consume observations as a temporally ordered history, with positional encodings tying each observation to when it was seen, making it difficult to reuse experience from earlier traversals of an environment. Systems that do reuse such experience usually construct an explicit representation, such as a map or a topological graph, and plan on it. We propose VNT-PA (Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention), a transformer planner whose context is a set of depth keyframes indexed by camera pose. With camera poses as positional encoding, attention depends on the pose differences between keyframes rather than on their temporal order. VNT-PA is trained to imitate a shortest-path planner operating on the ground-truth scene mesh, predicting actions by querying the spatial context with only its current pose and the goal position. On point-goal navigation in HM3D validation scenes, VNT-PA reaches 93.3% success and 90.4% success weighted by path length (SPL), outperforming baselines that encode the same context as a temporal sequence or treat pose as an input feature, in both navigation performance and training efficiency. Because the spatial context is a pose-indexed set, frames from different trajectories can be fused at test time. The planner also degrades more gracefully under localization noise than a conventional baseline which plans on explicit maps. These results show that pose-stamped experience can serve directly as the environment representation for a learned planner, and that making attention depend on pose differences, rather than on temporal order, speeds up training and improves long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [192] [Fewer Steps, Better Actions: Rethinking Flow-Matching Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21216)
*Zhipeng Tang,Xinda Chen,Weining Rao,Xiao Li,Wenting Tan,Yuning Wang,Xiao Shi,Xiaofang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Coda，将流匹配 VLA 策略的部分积分预算重分配给单次学习式端点校正，在冻结策略上训练轻量 Transformer 预测残差，从而改善成功率与推理延迟的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流匹配的 VLA 策略通过反复调用动作专家来生成动作块，增加积分步数会提高推理成本，却不一定提升闭环成功率。因此作者希望找到一种比单纯增加积分步数更有效的替代方案。

Method: 冻结的策略先执行少步的噪声到动作轨迹；随后一个轻量 Transformer 利用候选动作、源噪声以及共享的观测前缀缓存，预测一个由演示监督的残差进行端点校正。训练时仅训练校正器，策略本身保持冻结。

Result: 在 50 个 RoboTwin Easy 任务上，五步 Coda 将成功率从 71.64% 提升到 74.68%，同时相比默认十步策略降低 30.2% 的前向延迟；两步配置达到 71.88% 成功率并实现 2.12 倍加速。独立 13 任务对照在几乎相同延迟下取得 5.69 个百分点提升。该设计也提升了冻结的官方 SmolVLA，将两步成功率从 60.8% 提升到 69.4%。

Conclusion: 端点校正可改善冻结流匹配策略的质量—延迟权衡，是替代额外积分步骤的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies based on flow matching generate action chunks through repeated evaluations of an action expert. Increasing the number of integration steps raises inference cost, but does not necessarily improve closed-loop success. We propose Coda, which reallocates part of this integration budget to a single learned endpoint correction. A frozen policy first completes a few-step noise-to-action trajectory; a lightweight Transformer then predicts a demonstration-supervised residual using the candidate action, source noise, and shared observation-prefix cache. Only the corrector is trained. On 50 RoboTwin Easy tasks, five-step Coda improves success from 71.64% to 74.68% over the matched five-step baseline, while reducing forward latency by 30.2% relative to the default ten-step policy. A two-step configuration achieves 71.88% success with a 2.12$\times$ speedup. An independent 13-task control shows a 5.69-percentage-point gain at nearly equal latency, supporting correction as an effective alternative to additional integration. The same design also improves frozen official SmolVLA, raising two-step success from 60.8% to 69.4%. These results show that endpoint correction improves the quality-latency trade-off of frozen flow-matching policies.

</details>


### [193] [Safe Real-Time Policy Steering via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization for One-Step Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21220)
*Qingyi Chen,Joseph Ruan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INSPO 将一步生成策略的推理时引导表述为策略输入噪声空间中的轨迹优化，在诱导状态轨迹上评估约束，无需修改生成动作，通过种群粒子优化在线求解，在多个任务上提升了成功率与约束满足度且运行时间更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略能表达多样多模态行为，但预训练策略在部署时需满足避障、保持朝向等约束。现有推理时引导方法通常依赖迭代扩散或流过程的梯度引导，计算开销大，难以满足实时控制需求。

Method: 提出 INSPO，把一步生成策略的推理时引导建模为策略输入噪声空间中的轨迹优化。优化输入噪声，同时在诱导出的状态轨迹上评估约束，从而在策略诱导的行为空间中搜索，而不直接修改生成动作。优化目标包含正则项以保持解与策略输入分布一致，并采用基于种群（population-based particle optimization）的在线优化求解。

Result: 在 Push-T、Can pick-and-place 和 LIBERO-Spatial 上评估，覆盖基于状态与基于图像的任务专用策略以及通用视觉-语言-动作策略。INSPO 相比 best-of-N 采样与动作投影提升了任务成功率和约束满足度，并以更低运行时间优于梯度引导生成方法。

Conclusion: INSPO 提供了一种高效的一步生成策略推理时引导方案，通过在输入噪声空间进行轨迹优化实现约束满足，兼顾性能与实时性。

Abstract: Generative robot policies can represent diverse, multimodal behaviors, but adapting pretrained policies to deployment-time constraints such as collision avoidance and orientation maintenance remains challenging. Existing inference-time steering methods typically apply gradient guidance through iterative diffusion or flow processes, which can be computationally expensive for real-time control. We propose INSPO, which formulates inference-time steering of one-step generative policies as trajectory optimization in the policy's input noise space. By optimizing the input noise while evaluating constraints on the induced state trajectory, INSPO searches the policy-induced behavior space without directly modifying generated actions. The optimization includes a regularization term that encourages solutions to remain consistent with the policy's input distribution and is solved online using population-based particle optimization. We evaluate INSPO on state- and image-based task-specific policies and generalist vision-language-action policies across Push-T, Can pick-and-place, and LIBERO-Spatial. INSPO improves task success and constraint satisfaction over best-of-N sampling and action projection, while comparing favorably with gradient-guided generation at lower runtime.

</details>


### [194] [SafeStage: Evaluating Safety Before, During, and After Vision-Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21223)
*Jinzhu Luo,Qi Zhang,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SafeStage，一个按生命周期（执行前/中/后）划分的机器人操作安全评测基准，含97个风险场景，将任务成功率与安全结果解耦，揭示标称成功常与安全违规共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人策略评测多聚焦任务成功、单一物理约束、语义拒绝或已发生的物理损伤，难以定位闭环操作中安全失效发生在何处。因此需要一个能同时区分"任务是否完成"与"安全是否合规"，并定位违规发生时机的统一诊断基准。

Method: SafeStage 将评测划分为三个生命周期阶段：初始状态危险（操作目标前必须解决的安全相关关系）、执行期安全（执行中的不安全接触、轨迹、区域进入与物体交互）、最终状态危险（任务名义完成后仍存在的不稳定或不安全状态）。共构建97个专门设计的风险场景，采用基于事件与基于状态的检查评估实际发生的交互，并将任务成功与各阶段安全结果分开报告。在统一的闭环协议下评测代表性的直接动作 VLA 策略与基于世界模型的策略。

Result: 实验表明，名义任务完成常常与安全违规同时存在；不同策略在三个阶段呈现各自不同的失效画像，说明仅看任务成功率无法反映安全水平。

Conclusion: 通过将任务成功与安全解耦，并定位违规发生的时间阶段，SafeStage 为视觉-语言条件机器人操作策略的评估与改进提供了统一诊断测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language-conditioned robot policies integrate perception, language understanding, and control for general-purpose manipulation. However, existing evaluations often focus on task success, isolated physical constraints, semantic refusal, or realized physical damage, providing limited insight into where safety fails during closed-loop manipulation. We introduce SafeStage, a lifecycle-structured benchmark for evaluating manipulation safety before, during, and after task execution. SafeStage contains 97 purpose-built risk scenarios organized into three stages. Initial-State Hazards captures safety-relevant relations that must be resolved before manipulating the target. Execution-Time Safety evaluates unsafe contacts, trajectories, region entries, and object interactions during execution. Final-State Hazards capture unstable or otherwise unsafe conditions remaining after nominal task completion. The benchmark evaluates realized interactions using event-based and state-based checks and reports native task success independently from stage-specific safety outcomes. We evaluate representative direct-action Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies and policies with world-model-based policies under a common closed-loop protocol. Our results demonstrate that nominal task completion frequently coexists with safety violations and that different policies exhibit distinct failure profiles across the three stages. By separating task success from safety and localizing when violations occur, SafeStage provides a unified diagnostic testbed for evaluating and improving vision-language-conditioned robot manipulation policies.

</details>


### [195] [AirSplan: Risk-Aware Motion Planning for Quadrotors in Cluttered 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21226)
*Seth Isaacson,William Hong,Katherine A. Skinner,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirSplan系统，结合归一化3D高斯泼溅场景表示与基于可达性的运动规划器，实现四旋翼无人机在复杂场景中的无碰撞路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机在农业、基础设施巡检等应用中需在复杂场景中严格避障，微小碰撞即可能导致机器人损毁。这带来两个挑战：一是在无真实障碍物数据时仍需高保真表示场景几何，二是规划器需利用该表示求出无碰撞路径。

Method: 采用3D高斯泼溅的归一化变体来编码高保真场景几何；并应用一种新颖的基于可达性的运动规划器，利用四旋翼的微分平坦特性计算连续时间碰撞约束，从而对机器人占据空间进行紧致过近似。

Result: 在具有挑战性的测试用例中，AirSplan成功找到路径的比例为81.2%，显著优于最接近基线方法的51.2%。

Conclusion: AirSplan有效解决了复杂场景表示与无碰撞运动规划这两项互补挑战，在成功率上大幅超越基线方法，验证了该系统的有效性。

Abstract: Quadrotors are increasingly deployed in applications such as agriculture, infrastructure inspection, and maintenance. In each of these applications, the robot must navigate complex scene geometry while remaining strictly collision-free. Unlike in ground domains, even minor collisions for aerial vehicles can result in the loss of the robot. This safety requirement induces a pair of technical challenges. First, the environment must be represented with sufficient fidelity to encode complex structure, even when no ground-truth obstacle data is available. Second, a motion planner must leverage this representation to determine a collision-free path to the goal. This paper proposes a system that addresses these complementary challenges. The proposed method, AirSplan, adopts a normalized variant of 3D Gaussian Splatting that encodes high-fidelity scene geometry. It then applies a novel reachability-based motion planner that leverages the differential flatness of quadrotors to compute continuous-time collision constraints that tightly overapproximate the robot's occupancy. Experiments demonstrate that AirSplan successfully finds a path in 81.2% of challenging test cases, a significant improvement over the nearest baseline method's 51.2%.

</details>


### [196] [FOCAL-VLA: Subtask-Guided Geometry Distillation and Implicit World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21228)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Di Wen,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Kunyu Peng,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FOCAL-VLA框架，通过子任务引导的几何蒸馏与隐式世界建模，提升视觉-语言-动作模型在精细和长时程操作任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型直接将当前2D观测映射为动作，缺乏足够的空间与时间理解；虽有方法引入几何监督和未来状态预测，但冗余场景信息会干扰与当前交互相关的几何和动态学习。

Method: 提出FOCAL-VLA：1）子任务引导的几何蒸馏，将VGGT的几何知识迁移到VLA模型，通过将几何潜变量与子任务相关图像区域特征对齐；2）隐式世界建模，利用当前与未来示范帧的Track4World特征捕捉当前交互的未来3D演化；两种表征联合指导动作生成，且推理时无需运行VGGT或Track4World。

Result: FOCAL-VLA在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: FOCAL-VLA通过聚焦子任务相关几何与动态信息，有效增强了VLA模型的空间与时间理解能力，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models built on pretrained vision-language models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, VLA models that directly map current 2D observations to actions often lack sufficient spatial and temporal understanding, limiting their performance in precise and long-horizon manipulation. Recent methods enhance VLA models through geometric supervision and future-state prediction across the entire scene. However, these methods can suffer from redundant scene information, distracting the model from learning the geometry and dynamics relevant to the current interaction. To address this issue, we propose FOCAL-VLA, a framework that combines subtask-guided geometry distillation with implicit world modeling to learn representations of current spatial structure and future interaction dynamics. To focus geometric learning on the current subtask, we transfer geometric knowledge from VGGT to the VLA model by aligning geometry latents with features from subtask-relevant image regions. To capture the future 3D evolution of the current interaction, we incorporate implicit world modeling using Track4World features from current and future demonstration frames. The two complementary representations jointly guide action generation without running VGGT or Track4World at inference time. Experiments show that FOCAL-VLA outperforms baselines on both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. Project website: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/FOCAL-VLA/.

</details>


### [197] [KnowDemo: Knowledge-Guided Robot Demonstration Generation from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21229)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KnowDemo框架，利用人类视频中的结构化操作知识，为目标工作空间生成多样化机器人演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人演示数据采集成本高；现有从人类视频生成演示的方法以运动参考适配为核心，会保留原始接触策略与子任务顺序，限制行为多样性；同时对任务需求与场景关系理解不足，会产生大量无效候选，降低生成效率。

Method: 基于VLM构建知识提取与推理模块，将物体与动作描述同推断的任务条件、演示参考及允许的执行变化相关联，以区分任务需求与演示特定选择；将知识解析到目标场景实体与几何上，指导候选生成与筛选，再进行运动规划与仿真验证。

Result: 生成的演示通过替代接触策略与有效子任务顺序呈现多模态行为，并带有结构化执行标签；相比仅参考配置，产生额外经验证的执行模式；任务引导的抓取采样提升了候选规划成功率；在仿真数据上微调预训练π_{0.5}模型，实现三任务的仿真到真实迁移。

Conclusion: 结构化操作知识能有效提升机器人演示生成的多样性与效率，并支持策略学习的仿真到真实迁移。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies typically requires substantial demonstration data, which are costly to collect on real robots. Recent methods generate robot demonstrations from human videos by adapting recovered motion and validating the resulting trajectories in simulation. However, methods centered on motion-reference adaptation can limit behavioral diversity by retaining the demonstrated contact strategies and subtask orders, while insufficient understanding of task requirements and scene relations can reduce demonstration generation efficiency by generating invalid candidates. To address these limitations, we propose KnowDemo, a framework that uses structured manipulation knowledge from human videos to generate diverse robot demonstrations for a target workspace. To distinguish task requirements from demonstration-specific choices, we develop a knowledge extraction and reasoning module based on a vision-language model (VLM) that associates object and action descriptions with inferred task conditions, demonstration references, and permissible execution variations. To translate this knowledge into executable demonstrations, we resolve the descriptions against target-scene entities and geometry to guide candidate generation and screening before motion planning and simulation. The resulting demonstrations exhibit multimodal behavior through alternative contact strategies and valid subtask orders, with structured execution labels. Experiments demonstrate additional verified execution modes beyond a reference-only configuration and improved candidate planning success through task-guided grasp sampling. To validate the generated data for policy learning, we fine-tune the pretrained $π_{0.5}$ model on simulation data, achieving sim-to-real transfer across three tasks. Project page: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/knowdemo/

</details>


### [198] [VLA-Scope: Shift-Aware Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21246)
*Kaiwen Zhu,Dongfang Liu,Liangkai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Scope是一个两阶段框架，结合输入偏移表征与执行历史，在分布外（OOD）场景下预测机器人执行失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在分布偏移下可靠性下降。但由于模型在OOD条件下仍可能成功，仅检测OOD输入不足以预测执行失败，因此需要结合执行历史来预测失败风险。

Method: 第一阶段利用池化的图像与语言表征检测OOD输入并分类其偏移类别。第二阶段针对被标记为OOD的输入，结合预测类别、动作前缀特征与执行进度特征，使用跨偏移类别共享的逻辑回归模型随执行推进更新失败风险。基于OpenVLA在十个LIBERO-Spatial任务上采用留一组交叉验证进行评估。

Result: OOD检测ROC-AUC达0.9454，偏移分类准确率91%。在全部1400次OOD rollout上独立评估，执行60步动作后失败预测ROC-AUC为0.8497，而无执行进度特征时为0.7906；且优于ActProbe与SAFE-MLP基线。

Conclusion: 将动作特征与时间聚合的执行步表征相结合，可提升输入偏移条件下的失败预测性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but distribution shifts can compromise their reliability. Because these models may still succeed under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions, detecting OOD inputs alone is insufficient to predict execution failure. In this paper, we introduce VLA-Scope, a two-stage framework that combines input-shift characterization with execution history to predict failure during OOD rollouts. The first stage uses pooled image and language representations to detect OOD inputs and classify their shift categories. For inputs flagged as OOD, the second stage combines the predicted category, action-prefix features, and execution progress features. A logistic regression model shared across shift categories updates failure risk as execution proceeds. We evaluate the framework with OpenVLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial tasks using leave-one-group-out cross-validation. OOD detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.9454, and shift classification achieves 91% accuracy. Evaluated independently of the OOD gate on all 1,400 OOD rollouts, the failure predictor achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.8497 after 60 executed actions, compared with 0.7906 without execution progress features. It also achieves a higher ROC-AUC than the evaluated ActProbe and SAFE-MLP baselines. These results suggest that combining action features with temporally aggregated execution step representations improves failure prediction under input shifts.

</details>


### [199] [LOInK: Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics via Structured Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21275)
*Michael Somerfield,Damian Abood,Ruigang Wang,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOInK 学习从构型空间到解耦任务/潜空间的双 Lipschitz 可逆映射，并将代价最小解置于原点，从而通过基于算子分裂的网络反演高效生成近似最优逆运动学解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学问题通常需要求解代价最小的解，而传统约束优化方法计算开销大、速度慢；已有生成式方法则需要大量采样并评估候选解才能得到高质量结果。因此作者希望找到一种既能保证解的近似最优性、又能高效采样的方法。

Method: 提出 LOInK 方法：在包含采样构型、对应任务变量以及给定代价函数的数据上训练，学习一个从构型空间到解耦的任务/潜空间的双 Lipschitz 可逆映射。潜空间被结构化设计，使代价最小的解位于原点，随后利用基于算子分裂（operator splitting）的网络反演算法高效采样代价最小解。

Result: 在三个问题上验证：1）一个用于说明的三自由度机械臂问题；2）四足攀爬机器人，LOInK 生成近最优解平均比约束优化方法快 31 倍，最快可达 100 倍；3）一个模拟软体驱动器，作为纯数据驱动示例，LOInK 能显式生成高质量解，而现有生成式方法需要多样采样和候选解评估。

Conclusion: LOInK 通过可逆映射与结构化潜空间，将逆运动学的最优解生成转化为原点附近的采样问题，实现了高效且高质量的近似最优解生成，在机器人控制与纯数据驱动场景中均优于传统约束优化和已有生成式方法。

Abstract: We introduce Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics (LOInK), a method to generate approximately optimal solutions to an inverse kinematics problem. When trained on data consisting of sampled configurations and associated task variables and a given cost function, LOInK learns a bi-Lipschitz invertible mapping from configuration space to a decoupled task/latent space, and moreover, the latent space is structured so as to place cost-minimizing solutions at the origin. This enables efficient sampling of cost-minimizing solutions via a network-inversion algorithm based on operator splitting. We demonstrate the proposed approach on three problems: an illustrative three degree-of-freedom manipulator problem; a quadrupedal climbing robot for which LOInK can generate near-optimal solutions on average 31 times faster and up to 100 times faster than a constrained optimization approach; and a simulated soft actuator as a purely data-driven example, in which LOInK can explicitly generate high-quality solutions, unlike existing generative approaches that require diverse sampling and evaluation of candidate solutions.

</details>


### [200] [Stability-aware Residual Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robotic Manipulator Disturbance Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21307)
*Jihong Kim,Joonhyuk Kwon,Hwa Soo Kim,TaeWon Seo,Hyung-Tae Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出残差强化学习扰动观测器框架，将解析观测器与RL策略结合，并用ISS分析给出状态相关动作约束以保证稳定性，实现零样本仿真到现实的精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器与扰动观测器在机械臂精密跟踪中受限于参数不确定性、非线性摩擦和复合扰动，模型难以完全刻画这些效应，需要补偿模型无法捕获的残差。

Method: 以确定性解析观测器为基线，运行于可靠区域；RL策略专门补偿其残余误差。引入估计器网络，将观测历史与特权扰动上下文对齐，使潜空间按扰动模式组织，实现扰动切换时的快速适应。基于输入到状态稳定性（ISS）分析推导并施加状态相关的动作上界，使闭环跟踪误差对任意策略输出都被约束在可认证包络内。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂上，扰动估计与跟踪性能均持续提升：零样本仿真到现实迁移下真实硬件跟踪误差降低27.8%，在训练中未出现的基座振动扰动下降低38.0%。

Conclusion: 残差RL与解析观测器结合、并配合ISS导出的动作约束，能在保证稳定性的同时有效补偿未建模扰动，且具备良好的仿真到现实泛化能力。

Abstract: Although conventional controllers and disturbance observers (DOBs) are the standard for precision tracking in manipulators, they suffer from parameter uncertainty, nonlinear friction, and compound disturbances. This study proposes a residual reinforcement learning DOB framework that pairs an analytical observer with an RL policy. The deterministic baseline operates within a reliable region, whereas the RL policy explicitly targets the residuals that the model cannot capture. To make this compensation disturbance-aware, an estimator network aligns the observation history with a privileged disturbance context, organizing the latent space by disturbance regime and enabling rapid adaptation across disturbance transitions. To guarantee stability, we derived and enforced a state-dependent action bound on the RL policy from an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis such that the closed loop provably confines the tracking error to a certified envelope for arbitrary policy outputs. Experiments on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrated consistent improvements in disturbance estimation and tracking, including a 27.8% tracking-error reduction on real hardware under zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and a 38.0% reduction under a base-vibration disturbance that was not observed during training.

</details>


### [201] [NaViRrator: Robot Navigation from Human-Readable Maps through a Learned Visual Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21316)
*Ayun Lee,Jiseon Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NaViRRator框架，通过RouteScribe将地图上的起终点翻译为导航指令，核心是用SGL-CFM生成显式路线骨架再由VLM转成语言指令，供预训练VLN策略执行，真机成功率与SPL均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读地图提供直观的机器人目的地指定界面，但其示意性几何与机器人第一视角观测之间的连接仍然困难，直接由地图生成指令存在路线不清晰、操作顺序难以保持的问题。

Method: RouteScribe将路线推断与语言表达解耦：先用起终点连线条件流匹配（SGL-CFM）把直线航点序列变形为地图条件下的显式路线骨架，再用预训练视觉语言模型把骨架转成导航指令；执行时VLN策略仅接收指令与第一视角观测，地图与骨架留在上游。

Result: 真实世界实验中成功率与SPL均高于直接地图到指令生成、基于A*的骨架生成以及高斯源条件流匹配；定性结果显示显著转弯更清晰、目标操作序列保持更好。

Conclusion: 以路线为中介的语言可作为人类可读地图与预训练导航策略之间的模块化接口，且执行器可替换而无需重训地图到语言模块。

Abstract: Human-readable maps provide an intuitive interface for specifying robot destinations, but connecting their schematic geometry to egocentric observations remains challenging. We present NaViRRator, a framework that translates user-specified start and goal locations on such maps into navigation instructions for a pretrained vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policy. Its core method, RouteScribe, separates route inference from verbalization by first generating an explicit route scaffold in map-image coordinates, which a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) converts into a navigation instruction. We construct the scaffold with start--goal line conditional flow matching (SGL-CFM), which deforms a straight start--goal waypoint sequence into a map-conditioned route. During execution, the VLN policy receives only the instruction and egocentric observations, while the map and scaffold remain upstream, allowing executor replacement without retraining the map-to-language modules. Real-world experiments show higher success rates and success weighted by path length (SPL) than direct map-to-instruction generation, A*-based scaffolding, and Gaussian-source conditional flow matching. Qualitative results further show clearer salient turns and better preservation of the intended maneuver sequence, supporting route-grounded language as a modular interface between human-readable maps and pretrained navigation policies.

</details>


### [202] [LEMCA: LLM-Guided Synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21319)
*Arjun Krishna,Vincent Pacelli,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LEMCA，用大语言模型自动合成高效的"模式切换控制架构"（MSC），通过进化循环迭代优化可解释的程序化设计，在多种控制任务上超越单体设计的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界的物理控制任务（如驾驶、物体操控）在时间上对传感与计算复杂度的需求差异巨大，因此动态切换不同资源分配的控制模式是资源高效的自然选择。但传统 MSC 需要针对每个新任务进行大量专家驱动的设计与合成，导致现代机器人控制架构退回到浪费资源的"单体式"一刀切结构，资源分配永久锚定在最困难、最耗资源的任务阶段。

Method: 提出 LEMCA（LLM-Guided synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures）。它将 MSC 设计表示为可解释的程序，在进化循环中迭代精炼；为评估设计适配度，提出兼容 MSC 的自动控制器合成扩展（如仿真中的强化学习）；并利用 LLM 的语义先验、推理与编码能力，迭代编辑控制器模式、其对应的传感-计算资源分配以及模式切换。

Result: 在多种控制基准上，LEMCA 能持续发现超越单体设计帕累托前沿的策略，通过回收"简单"任务阶段中浪费的资源来提升效率。

Conclusion: LEMCA 提供了一条自动化、低人工成本的路径，用于合成资源高效的模式切换控制架构设计。

Abstract: Physical control tasks in the natural world, such as driving or object manipulation, frequently exhibit dramatic variations in sensory and compute complexity over time. Correspondingly, a natural resource-efficient choice for robot control is to dynamically switch between control modes with varying resource allocations. However, such "mode-switching controllers" (MSCs) have historically required laborious, expert-driven design and synthesis for each new task. Driven by these design difficulties, modern robotic control architectures often fall back to a wasteful "monolithic" one-size-fits-all structure, where resource allocation is permanently anchored to the hardest, most resource-intensive task phases. To facilitate the design of performant yet efficient MSCs, we propose LLM-Guided synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures (LEMCA). LEMCA represents MSC designs as interpretable programs to be iteratively refined in an evolutionary loop. To evaluate design fitness, we propose MSC-compatible extensions of automated controller synthesis approaches, such as reinforcement learning in simulation. LEMCA then leverages the semantic priors, reasoning, and coding capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively edit controller modes, their corresponding sensory-compute resource allocations, and mode transitions. Our experiments across diverse control benchmarks show that LEMCA consistently discovers strategies that surpass the Pareto frontier of monolithic designs by reclaiming wasted resources during "easy" task phases. LEMCA thus presents an automated, low-effort path to synthesize resource-efficient MSC designs.

</details>


### [203] [The EventCV Library for Event-Based Robotic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21330)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EventCV是一个开源可扩展的Rust库（带OpenCV风格Python绑定），提供从去噪、特征学习、运动估计到仿真和ONNX推理的一站式事件相机处理工具，文件解码速度比现有库快1.1–3.7倍，并在Jetson Orin AGX上完成三项机器人案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级异步响应、高动态范围和低功耗等优势，适合快速运动或恶劣光照下的机器人应用。但将其集成到实际机器人流程中仍障碍重重：缺乏即插即用驱动、事件流文件格式互不兼容、多数项目依赖定制的研究级代码，导致入门门槛高。

Method: 作者开发了EventCV，一个开源、可扩展的Rust库，并提供OpenCV风格的Python绑定。功能涵盖去噪滤波、几何变换、数据增强、角点检测与无监督特征学习、对比度最大化运动估计、视频到事件流的仿真器，以及面向机器人部署的ONNX推理。该库集成了Neuromorphic Drivers包，可实现事件相机流的实时直接处理。

Result: 现有工具箱无法在单一包中覆盖如此广泛的操作，而EventCV在表示构建和文件解码上比现有库快1.1x至3.7x。作者在Jetson Orin AGX上部署EventCV，并展示了涵盖目标检测、设备端模型推理和定位的三项机器人案例研究。

Conclusion: EventCV通过统一、高效、可扩展的工具链降低了事件相机的研究与应用门槛，并验证了其在真实机器人系统中的实用性，相关资源见项目网页 https://eventcv.net。

Abstract: Event cameras detect per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously on microsecond timescales, with high dynamic range and low power draw. These are desirable properties for robots that move fast or work in difficult lighting conditions. However, integrating an event camera into a real-world robotic pipeline still requires substantial effort: plug-and-play drivers do not exist, event streams are recorded in a variety of incompatible file formats, and most projects rely on custom research-grade code. Here, we present EventCV, an open-source and extensible Rust library with OpenCV-style Python bindings that lowers the entry barrier to working with event cameras. EventCV provides a wide range of features: denoising filters and geometric transforms, augmentations, corner detection and unsupervised feature learning, contrast-maximization motion estimation, a video-to-events simulator, and Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) inference for deployment in robotic stacks. EventCV integrates the Neuromorphic Drivers package, allowing an event camera stream to be processed directly in real time. No existing toolkit covers this range of operations in one package, and EventCV builds representations and decodes files 1.1x to 3.7x faster than the currently available libraries. We deploy EventCV on a Jetson Orin AGX and present three robotics case studies spanning object detection, on-device model inference, and localization. Project webpage: https://eventcv.net.

</details>


### [204] [FAN: Foresight Action Normalization for Continual Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21358)
*Yijun Hong,Jiarun Zhu,Xiaoquan Sun,Le Xu,Qijun He,Xin Jin,Mingqi Yuan,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了持续VLA适配中的动作归一化策略，提出一致性、覆盖性、因果性（3C）设计原则及前瞻动作归一化（FAN）方法，在四个真实任务流上取得最优性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型在机器人操作任务中表现优异，但长期真实部署需持续获取新技能并保留旧能力。已有持续VLA适配研究（如经验回放、强化微调）忽略了动作归一化这一基础机制——它决定策略感知与执行动作的坐标系，现有协议会导致任务间坐标漂移、运动覆盖不足或训练-测试坐标不匹配等严重失效。

Method: 系统评估五种归一化策略，覆盖单臂与双臂操作的四个真实任务流；归纳出一致性、覆盖性、因果性三条核心设计原则（3C）；提出前瞻动作归一化FAN——在持续学习前从一个小型、任务无关的校准集估计一次归一化统计量，并在整个适配过程中冻结不变。

Result: 在所有评估任务流中，FAN取得最高性能并展现一致稳健性，明显优于现有归一化协议。

Conclusion: FAN为构建稳定的动作表示、实现有效的终身VLA适配提供了有指导意义的方案与设计准则。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on large-scale, closed datasets have demonstrated remarkable success across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, their long-term real-world deployment necessitates continuously acquiring new skills while retaining previously learned capabilities. While pioneering works have explored continual VLA adaptation using techniques such as experience replay and reinforcement fine-tuning, they overlook a foundational mechanism: action normalization, which determines the underlying coordinate system in which policies perceive and execute physical actions. To bridge this gap, we systematically evaluate five normalization strategies across four real-world task streams covering single-arm and bimanual manipulation. Our analysis reveals that existing protocols induce severe failure modes due to inter-task coordinate drift, limited motion coverage, or train-test coordinate mismatches. Motivated by these insights, we formulate three core design principles: consistency, coverage, and causality (3C), and introduce foresight action normalization (FAN). FAN estimates normalization statistics once from a small, task-independent calibration set prior to continual learning and freezes them throughout adaptation. Across all evaluated streams, FAN achieves the highest performance and demonstrates consistent robustness, providing insightful guidance for building stable action representations in achieving effective lifelong VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [205] [MicroHookACT: Monocular Microscopic Vision Guided Visuomotor Policy for Flexible Microelectrode Hooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21365)
*Yitong Chen,Fangbo Qin,Yang Wang,Ruihua Hu,Kui Zhang,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MicroHookACT，一种基于模仿学习的视觉运动策略，在单目显微视觉下实现柔性微电极植入中微针-微环的自动化3D钩取，总成功率96.7%，平均执行时间11.5秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性微电极（FME）植入中，自动化针-环钩取是关键步骤，但现有方法在单目显微视觉下难以实现无触诊的精确对准与接触丰富的穿线，缺乏对微米级操作的可靠控制。

Method: 1）单向钩取策略：利用离焦线索与光轴引导，实现无触诊精确对准与接触丰富的穿线；2）动作监督目标注意力模块：基于冻结ViT主干，从人类演示中直接学习聚焦微针尖与微环，无需人工视觉标注；3）以注意力为中心的全局粗特征与局部细特征，按预测动作进度动态加权，使单一ACT策略适应操作中变化的离焦模糊与视觉需求。

Result: 在60条人类演示上训练，于5种不同难度设置下评估；MicroHookACT取得最高整体成功率96.7%，平均执行时间11.5秒。

Conclusion: 结果证明了视觉运动策略学习在多变操作条件下实现微米级控制的潜力。

Abstract: Automated needle-loop hooking is a critical step in flexible microelectrode (FME) implantation. This paper presents MicroHookACT, an imitation learning-based visuomotor policy for automated 3D hooking under monocular microscopic vision. First, a unidirectional hooking strategy exploits defocus cues and optical-axis guidance to enable palpation-free precise alignment and contact-rich threading. Second, an action-supervised object attention module built on a frozen ViT backbone learns to focus on the micro-needle tip and micro-loop directly from human demonstrations, without requiring manual visual annotations for training. Third, attention-centered global coarse and local fine features are dynamically weighted according to predicted action progress, enabling a single ACT policy to adapt to changing defocus blur and visual requirements throughout the operation. In the experiments, visuomotor policies were trained on 60 human demonstrations and evaluated under five setups with varying difficulties. Our MicroHookACT framework achieved the highest overall success rate of 96.7\% with an average execution time of 11.5 s. These results demonstrate the potential of visuomotor policy learning for micron-level control under varying operating conditions.

</details>


### [206] [ProTracer: Proprioception-Guided Failure Diagnosis in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21369)
*Chang Dong,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Francois Fraysse,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ProTracer，一个免训练框架，利用视觉语言模型与本体感知信号进行机器人操作失败分析，涵盖失败检测、分类、解释生成及失败起始定位，并引入FailTime基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的失败分析至关重要，但现有方法多聚焦于传统失败诊断任务，缺乏对细粒度时间维度的失败分析，尤其是识别机器人最早偏离有效任务完成轨迹的失败起始时刻这一能力。

Method: ProTracer是一个免训练框架：利用本体感知动态识别时间上有信息量的动作边界，并将丰富的机器人状态信号转换为结构化自然语言描述，与视觉观察一起交由视觉语言模型联合分析，从而结合本体感知信号的时间精度与现代VLM的多模态推理能力，无需额外模型训练。

Result: 实验表明ProTracer在传统失败诊断任务和新增的失败起始定位任务上均取得强劲性能，凸显了本体感知推理对细粒度时间失败分析的重要性。

Conclusion: 本体感知信号与VLM的结合能有效提升机器人操作失败分析的细粒度时间能力，免训练设计也提供了良好的可扩展性，FailTime基准为相关评测提供了支持。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive framework for robot manipulation failure analysis that includes binary failure detection, failure categorization, explanation generation, and the additional capability of failure onset localization, which aims to identify the earliest moment at which a robot execution deviates from a valid task-completion trajectory and is ultimately followed by task failure. To address these tasks, we propose ProTracer, a training-free framework that leverages existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with proprioceptive signals for failure analysis. Our method uses proprioceptive dynamics to identify temporally informative action boundaries and converts richer robot-state signals into structured natural-language descriptions that can be jointly analyzed together with visual observations by the VLM. This design combines the temporal precision of proprioceptive signals with the multimodal reasoning capabilities of modern VLMs without requiring additional model training. We further introduce FailTime, a benchmark with synchronized visual and proprioceptive observations for evaluating conventional failure diagnosis tasks as well as failure onset localization. Experiments demonstrate that ProTracer achieves strong performance across both conventional failure diagnosis tasks and the newly introduced failure onset localization task, highlighting the importance of proprioceptive reasoning for fine-grained temporal failure analysis.

</details>


### [207] [AVT-Fabric: Active Visuo-Tactile Perception via Adaptive Evidence Selection for Efficient Robotic Fabric Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21377)
*Chang Gao,Zhuo Chen,Suhang Xia,Jihong Zhu,Jiankang Deng,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AVT-Fabric是一个RGB优先的自适应视触觉织物比较框架，按比较难度动态分配触觉证据，以7B MLLM取得98.0%准确率，超越90B基线且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人织物比较需要联合视觉外观与触觉线索，但现有方法（如90B MLLM-Fabric）模型规模大、依赖固定被动推理流程，计算代价高且效率低，缺乏根据任务难度自适应选择证据的能力。

Method: 提出AVT-Fabric框架：采用双尺度门控（基于答案token置信度与原始logit分离度）判断是否需要额外的力标记GelSight触觉观测；用紧凑文本记忆保存执行历史；通过多数投票整合所选预测。

Result: 在400个保留比较上达98.0%准确率（7B MLLM），超越90B MLLM-Fabric基线的94.0%（+4.0个百分点），平均仅用五个阶段中的1.60个；相较匹配的被动推理提升9.25个百分点，模型端延迟降低61.8%，并优于纯RGB准确率。四个额外MLLM骨干验证了泛化性、准确性与效率；在真实机器人系统上达78.1%成对排序准确率，8个应用场景中7个正确选布。

Conclusion: 自适应证据选择能同时提升机器人视触觉推理的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Robotic fabric comparison needs to actively combine visual appearance and tactile cues. Here, we present AVT-Fabric, an RGB-first framework that allocates tactile evidence according to the difficulty of each comparison. A dual-scale gate evaluates answer-token confidence and raw logit separation to determine whether another force-tagged GelSight observation is needed. Compact textual memory preserves the executed history, and majority voting consolidates the selected predictions. On 400 held-out comparisons, AVT-Fabric achieves 98.0% accuracy with a compact 7B Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), surpassing the 94.0% reported by the 90B MLLM-Fabric baseline by 4.0 percentage points while processing only 1.60 of five available stages on average. It improves on matched passive inference by 9.25 percentage points and reduces model-side latency by 61.8%, while also improving on RGB-only accuracy. Four additional MLLM backbones support the generalizability, accuracy, and efficiency of the framework. This framework is also deployed on a real robotic system, achieving 78.1% pairwise ranking accuracy and correct fabric selection in seven of eight application scenarios. AVT-Fabric demonstrates that adaptive evidence selection can improve both the accuracy and efficiency of robotic visuo-tactile reasoning.

</details>


### [208] [MarineCraft: Enabling Rapid Prototyping of Underwater Robots via Modular Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21396)
*Yuta Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MarineCraft模块化工具包，通过自包含防水推进模块消除集中布线和反复密封需求，实现水下机器人数分钟内快速组装与原型迭代，可在2.5米深度可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人开发常受防水与布线复杂性的制约，显著拖慢了快速原型开发进程。作者希望降低水下机器人开发门槛，加速设计迭代。

Method: 设计MarineCraft模块化工具包，采用结构可重构方案，核心是自包含的防水推进模块，集成电源、无线通信与驱动功能，从而消除集中布线并免去重复密封。

Result: 实验表明该可重构架构支持快速迭代设计周期，同时保持可靠运行与无泄漏性能，可在最深2.5米的水下环境中工作，机器人几何构型可在数分钟而非数天内完成组装与测试。

Conclusion: 该工具包通过将模块化转化为快速物理原型的手段，有效降低了水下机器人开发的门槛。

Abstract: Underwater robot development is often hindered by the complexities of waterproofing and wiring, which significantly delay the rapid prototyping process. This paper presents MarineCraft, a modular toolkit designed to accelerate the development cycle through structural reconfiguration. The system features self-contained, waterproof propulsion modules that integrate power, wireless communication, and actuation. By eliminating centralized wiring and the need for repeated sealing, MarineCraft allows diverse robot geometries to be assembled and tested in minutes rather than days. Experimental results demonstrate that this reconfigurable architecture enables fast, iterative design cycles while maintaining reliable operation and leak-free performance at depths of up to 2.5 meters. Our toolkit effectively lowers the barrier to underwater robotics by transforming modularity into a vehicle for rapid physical prototyping.

</details>


### [209] [Stabilizing Trajectory Outputs in End-to-End Autonomous Driving via SC-IMM Based Teacher Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21404)
*Siewoo Kim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于场景条件交互多模型（SC-IMM）的离线教师信号生成与学习方法，用于端到端自动驾驶轨迹输出稳定化，在CARLA Town12闭环评测中显著提升驾驶分数并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于路点的模仿学习主要最小化坐标级误差，难以捕捉依赖场景的路径-速度变化以及路点输出在时间上的不稳定性，导致闭环行为不够稳定。

Method: 将专家轨迹转换为路径-速度状态，并基于场景线索进行IMM更新，生成路径-速度教师标签与模式后验概率；这些信号作为辅助监督加入原始轨迹损失中用于训练，而推理结构与路点控制器保持不变。

Result: 在CARLA Town12的100条短路线闭环评测中，相比基线驾驶分数提升28.0%，Collision/km降低62.3%，同时jerk和轨迹变化指标也有所改善。

Conclusion: 嵌入场景条件运动模型线索的离线教师信号，能够引导轨迹输出型驾驶模型获得更稳定的闭环行为。

Abstract: End-to-End autonomous driving models commonly predict future waypoints from sensor inputs and convert them into vehicle control commands through a downstream controller. However, conventional waypoint-based imitation learning mainly minimizes coordinate-level errors, making it difficult to capture scene-dependent path-speed changes and temporal instability across waypoint outputs. In this paper, we propose an offline teacher-signal generation and learning method for trajectory-output stabilization based on a Scene-Conditioned Interacting Multiple Model (SC-IMM) to mitigate this issue. The proposed method converts expert trajectories into path-speed states and performs IMM updates conditioned on scene cues to generate path-speed teacher labels and mode posterior probabilities. The generated signals are added to the original trajectory loss as auxiliary supervision during training, while the inference structure and waypoint controller remain unchanged. In closed-loop evaluation on 100 short routes in CARLA Town12, the proposed method improved the driving score by 28.0% and reduced Collision/km by 62.3% compared with the baseline, while also improving jerk and trajectory-variation metrics. These results demonstrate that offline teacher signals embedding scene-conditioned motion-model cues can guide trajectory-output driving models toward more stable closed-loop behavior.

</details>


### [210] [A Unified Dynamic Force Guidance Framework for Performance-Optimized Kinesthetic Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21416)
*Chunxin Li,Jianhua Wu,Zhenhua Xiong,Xiangyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线动态力引导方法，将性能约束与优化机制相结合，在拖动示教过程中提升协作机器人的可操作性能，从而提高工作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人在频繁换产的工业场景中应用增多，拖动示教可加快部署，但用户在示教时容易忽略机器人构型配置，导致可操作性能下降，进而影响运动生成能力与工作效率。

Method: 提出在线动态力引导方法：以雅可比矩阵最小奇异值量化可操作性能；采用变导纳控制在示教中维持性能高于预设阈值；同时引入虚拟力主动引导用户将机器人拖向性能更优的构型。

Result: 在6自由度协作机器人上对任务空间中三条典型路径进行对比实验，并通过轨迹回放实验分析可操作性能与工作效率的关系。结果表明所提方法有效提升了机器人可操作性能，并相应提高了工作效率。

Conclusion: 该方法对缩短工业生产节拍时间、促进工业部署具有重要价值。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly deployed in industrial scenarios characterized by frequent product changeovers. As an intuitive programming method, kinesthetic teaching facilitates rapid robot deployment. However, users may overlook the configuration of the robot during kinesthetic teaching, leading to degradation in operational performance. Operational performance refers to the capability of the robot to generate motion and can be quantified by the Minimum Singular Value of the Jacobian matrix. To address this issue, this paper proposes an online dynamic force guidance method that integrates performance constraint and optimization mechanisms. Specifically, variable admittance control maintains the operational performance of the robot above a predefined threshold, while a virtual force actively guides the user to drag the robot towards configurations with improved performance. Experiments are conducted on a 6-DOF collaborative robot, comparing three typical paths in the task space. To evaluate the quality of the taught trajectories, trajectory playback experiments are conducted to analyze the relationship between the operational performance of the robot and the work efficiency. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively enhances the operational performance of the robot and consequently improves the work efficiency, holding significant value for reducing production takt time in industrial deployment.

</details>


### [211] [FootQuery: Future-Touchdown-Guided Retrieval from Depth History for Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21447)
*Tao Dong,Jia Yu,Yuxuan Fan,Linna Zhao,Jiaqi Gong,Andong Yang,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FootQuery感知式人形运动框架，利用每只脚预测的下一触地点查询历史深度帧，实现复杂地形下的连续通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂地形的人形运动需要在落脚点可能已不可见时提前预判落脚位置。由于相机视野有限且存在自遮挡，必须从更早的观测中检索相关地形信息。

Method: 策略由本体感知预测落脚位置及不确定性，结合逐脚特征查询稀疏采样的历史深度帧；训练时将实际接触投影到历史图像中，监督在接触可见区域的信息检索；检索到的逐脚特征与全局视觉记忆融合生成控制动作。采用渐进式力辅助课程支持早期探索，并用事件一致的踏面中线塑形促进协调的楼梯接触。部署仅需本体感知与机载深度图像。

Result: 仿真中，完整框架在最困难的楼梯、间隙和平台测试上优于各组件消融版本；在Unitree G1上的真实世界实验中，单一策略实现户外楼梯与室内路线（含上下楼梯、平台和间隙）的连续通过。

Conclusion: 结果支持围绕预期接触来组织视觉历史，从而服务于感知式人形运动。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion over complex terrain requires anticipating footholds that may no longer be visible at touchdown. Limited camera coverage and self-occlusion make it necessary to retrieve relevant terrain information from earlier observations. We present FootQuery, a perceptive locomotion framework that queries depth history using each foot's predicted next touchdown. The policy predicts touchdown locations and uncertainty from proprioception and uses these distributions, together with per-foot features, to query sparsely sampled historical depth frames. During training, realized contacts are projected into historical images to supervise retrieval at the regions where those contacts were visible. The retrieved per-foot features are fused with global visual memory to generate control actions. A progressive force-assistance curriculum supports early exploration, while event-consistent tread-midline shaping encourages coordinated stair contacts. Deployment requires only proprioception and onboard depth images. In simulation, the complete framework outperforms its component ablations on the most challenging tested stairs, gaps, and platforms. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 demonstrate continuous traversal with a single policy across outdoor stairs and indoor routes combining stair ascent and descent, platforms, and gaps. These results support organizing visual history around anticipated contacts for perceptive humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [212] [Robotic Multiphase Interaction: Manipulating Coupled Liquid and Solid Dynamics with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21448)
*Yixuan Feng,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"机器人多相交互(RMI)"新任务设定,以含水海绵为首个案例,结合隐式不可压缩多孔流SPH仿真与机器人操作,并构建动作条件世界模型与时序UNet动作生成器,大幅降低持水预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究多针对纯固体对象,或仅让液固两相在空间上保持分离,缺乏对液体渗入可变形多孔骨架、二者相互耦合这一情形的建模;而此类操作会同时改变孔隙体积、挤出或重分布液体并影响抓取稳定性,且液体溢出在家庭与制造场景中存在安全隐患,因此需要专门研究。

Method: 以充水海绵作为首个RMI案例,采用隐式不可压缩多孔流与光滑粒子流体动力学(SPH)作为动力学引擎;通过引入库仑接触记忆、混合速度与力调节以及提升稳定性门控实现机器人操作,构建连接机器人指令与液固耦合状态的环境。进一步构建以动作为条件的世界模型预测状态演化,并用时序UNet以扩散策略或整流流匹配生成动作。

Result: 世界模型将持水量预测误差相比基线降低60%以上;由世界模型从策略提案中选出的最优动作序列,进一步将预测的终端水误差减少约一半。

Conclusion: 结果表明,对液固耦合状态进行建模有助于机器人预测其动作如何同时影响多孔物体与内部所持液体,为多相交互操作提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This work presents \textit{Robotic Multiphase Interaction (RMI)}, a setting in which liquid enters a porous material and interacts mechanically with its deforming solid skeleton. Manipulation can therefore change pore volume, expel or redistribute retained liquid, and alter grasp stability at the same time. Spilled liquid can also create safety risks in domestic and manufacturing settings. This differs from most manipulation of solid objects and from tasks that involve both liquid and solid while keeping the phases spatially separate. We study a sponge filled with water as the first RMI example. We use implicit incompressible porous flow with smoothed particle hydrodynamics as the dynamics engine and enable robotic manipulation by adding Coulomb contact memory, hybrid velocity and force regulation, and a stability gate for lifting. The resulting environment connects robot commands to changes in the coupled liquid and solid state. A world model conditioned on actions predicts how this state evolves under candidate commands, while a temporal UNet generates actions using either Diffusion Policy or rectified flow matching. Our world model reduces retained water prediction error by more than $60\%$ compared with the baseline. The best action sequence selected by the world model from policy proposals further reduces the predicted terminal water error by about half. These improvements show that modelling the coupled liquid and solid state helps the robot predict how its actions affect both the porous object and the liquid held inside.

</details>


### [213] [AtomEgo: Exploring Ego-Robot Integration for Embodied Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21461)
*Di Wu,Dongchen Zheng,Junhe Sheng,Zhongxing Wei,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiaoquan Sun,Junyang Zheng,Zhuoyang Song,Jiaxing Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AtomEgo，系统研究第一人称人类数据与机器人数据的协同预训练，发现"数据规模×对齐质量→能力增益"这一原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型受限于机器人演示数据的规模和多样性，因此希望利用大规模第一人称人类交互数据；但由于人与机器人在具身形态和动作空间上存在巨大差距，如何有效利用这类数据仍不明确。

Method: 构建约2,659小时的语料库和可扩展的数据处理流水线，在视觉-语言-动作与"世界-动作"两类模型架构上，研究三种代表性范式：带领域专用动作头的联合协同训练、通过具身对齐的渐进式"人→机器人"迁移，以及联合视频-动作建模。

Result: 通过多任务真机实验和语言条件下的跨具身表征分析进行评估，揭示出"数据规模 × 对齐质量 → 能力增益"的原则：第一人称数据可提升泛化能力，但其价值取决于对齐与利用的有效程度。

Conclusion: 该原则可为可扩展的人-机器人协同预训练提供实践指导。
