Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code https://gitlab.isir.upmc.fr/kappel/sim2real_public_chunk_control.

</details>


### [249] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对高功率扁平BLDC电机在动态机器人中的高转速需求，开发了基于GaN FET的紧凑型电机驱动器，实现96V下30A连续电流、80A峰值电流及3110Hz最高电频率，并验证了超额定范围高速区的延时补偿效果及动态机器人应用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态机器人需要快速能量输出，而商用伺服电机工作电压多在48V以下，限制了最高转速。为提升瞬时最大转速，需在超过额定电压下驱动电机。但半导体器件在耐压与电流容量间存在权衡，传统Si MOSFET驱动器难以兼顾高压与大电流，GaN FET虽有潜力，但可安装于扁平BLDC电机后侧的紧凑驱动器仍十分有限。

Method: 采用GaN FET开发适用于高功率扁平BLDC电机的电机驱动器，并研究其在超出额定工作范围的高速区域中的延时补偿效果。实验在96V工作条件下进行，配备散热片，并开展由24S LiPo电池（100V）驱动的高速负载提升实验。

Result: 在96V工作条件下，配合散热片实现了30A连续电流；确认峰值电流达80A，最高电频率为3110Hz；并通过24S LiPo电池（100V）驱动的高速负载提升实验，展示了其适用于动态机器人运行的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于GaN FET的紧凑型驱动器可有效突破低压伺服电机转速限制，兼顾高压与大电流，满足动态机器人对高瞬时速度与快速能量输出的需求。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [250] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 PopNavShift 仿真框架，通过大模型生成受人群特征调节的行人运动参数，在人群分布变化下压力测试三种社交导航策略，发现仅改变时间压力即可显著改变策略排名，且对行人负担指标的影响远大于对机器人通行时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航算法通常只在固定行人行为分布下评估，忽略了个体与社会情境带来的行人反应差异。若评估分布单一，可能得出对特定人群成立、但在人群变化时失效的结论，因此需要能模拟人群分布偏移的匹配式压力测试框架。

Method: 构建 PopNavShift：用 Gemini 3.7 Flash 处理来自 MatrAIx Persona 1M 的 600 条合成人格记录，生成受人群条件调节的行人运动画像，并确定性地映射为有界运动参数。随后在八种人群条件下比较三种代表性导航策略（反应式避让、提前让行、互惠避碰），共进行 7,488 次匹配的机器人运行；另在同一批 202 个行人画像上做匹配干预实验，仅改变时间压力，并用第二种行人动力学模型检验结果稳健性。

Result: 在匹配干预中，仅改变时间压力即导致控制器排名反转：按机器人通行时间衡量为 8.6%，按行人平均延误衡量为 22.4%，按最差十分位延误衡量为 23.9%。跨人群条件看，这种敏感性对行人负担的影响大于对机器人通行时间的影响，并在空间受限场景中增强；相同定性模式在第二种行人动力学模型下依然成立。

Conclusion: 导航策略的优劣排序高度依赖行人行为分布与情境因素，仅用机器人效率指标评估会产生误导。应跨行为人群评估导航策略，并同时报告机器人性能与行人负担两类指标。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [251] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIRGA，一种神经几何协调框架，将双LiDAR观测转化为有界、源特定的黎曼场，并通过带互惠弹性反馈的虚拟智能体进行耦合，实现无人机-地面车-云台空地对协同与持续可视观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当无人机必须持续处于地面车所搭载云台LiDAR的可观测范围内时，空地自主性变得更具挑战。平台需在单一闭环中同时应对动态障碍规避、异构运动协调、有限感知以及任务主导权变化等问题。

Method: VIRGA将双LiDAR观测转化为有界、源特定的黎曼场，并通过虚拟智能体以互惠弹性反馈耦合这些场。平台感知执行映射将共享协调参考转化为可行的无人机、地面车与云台指令，同时施加主动观测安全保护。此外引入在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介与互惠反馈等机制。

Result: 与三种基线对比：Ray-RMP控制器黎曼响应最快但耦合空地任务中净空不足；稠密解析黎曼场几何避障更好但评估成本过高，难以稳定维持视场；ColAG延迟最低但仍出现安全与可观测性违规。VIRGA安全完成所有成对仓库场景，并在无需重训练的远距离洞穴压力测试中，在不规则与受限几何下保持持续协调。消融实验验证了在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介与互惠反馈的贡献。

Conclusion: VIRGA通过神经几何协调与互惠反馈机制，有效解决了空地平台在保持观测约束下的协同问题，并在复杂受限环境中展现出良好的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [252] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CommitFlow：冻结基座策略的闭环执行框架，通过语义承诺监控与局部校正解决长时程执行的"语义承诺—物理状态"错配问题，在RoboTwin 2.0十项任务上成功率达75.9%，较pi0.5提升22.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略发展迅速，但长时程执行中常在所需物理效果尚未建立时就推进到下一任务阶段，即"语义承诺"（阶段须建立或维持的物理条件）与实际物理状态之间存在错配。由于单条动作指令无法确认该条件是否满足，局部偏差会逐级传播并导致任务失败。

Method: 提出CommitFlow闭环执行框架，保持基座策略冻结，融合承诺监控与局部校正，包含三部分：(1) Semantic Commitment Monitor（SCM），将阶段需求与当前状态证据比对，当必要条件未满足或被违反时，暂缓执行依赖后续动作；(2) BoundaryFlow，基于当前状态与基座动作生成局部校正；(3) Relation and Gain Calibration（RGC），在满足相关约束的前提下选择最小的校正强度。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的十项常见基准任务上，CommitFlow取得75.9%的平均成功率，相较基座策略pi0.5提升22.7%；跨策略实验显示增益具有一致性。

Conclusion: CommitFlow为实现可靠的机器人长时程执行提供了有效路径，在不改动基座策略的情况下通过闭环监控与轻度局部校正显著提升任务成功率，并具备跨策略泛化能力。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [253] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N. Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAAP与RAIL：将每条机械臂的手腕相机作为多智能体系统中的移动视角，配合角色感知模仿学习控制器，把协同操作本身转化为主动感知机制，显著提升多任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体操作天然产生多个任务驱动的视角（每条臂携带手腕相机并在动作中移动），但这些观测通常被低效利用，主动感知在操作中往往被当作需要专门感知智能体的独立任务，因此作者希望让操作臂兼具执行与感知双重角色。

Method: MAAP（Multi-Agent Active Perception）：每条机械臂既执行操作动作，又通过手腕相机成为团队的移动视角，实现多视角主动感知。RAIL（Role-Aware Imitation Learning）：控制器预测每条臂当前的角色并联合预测动作块，动作生成以角色为条件，从而在单一网络中表示角色相关的动作。

Result: 在四个仿真任务中，扩大感知范围使平均成功率从固定相机的56.5%提升至单活动手腕视角的62.5%、全部手腕视角的70.0%，MAAP+RAIL达到79.2%。RAIL的额外增益集中在三臂微波炉任务，相同多手腕输入下成功率由47%升至82%。双臂平台上MAAP+RAIL在20次放置试验中成功14次，而固定视角ACT为0次。

Conclusion: 协同操作本身即可作为一种主动感知机制，多机械臂的腕部视角与角色感知的动作生成相结合能大幅提升操作成功率，无需额外专用感知智能体。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [254] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARF 提出一种对比式"吸引-排斥"框架，通过进步重要性评分区分并利用失败轨迹中的有效片段与失败关键片段，实现从非完美机器人数据中更充分学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示收集常包含失败或不完美轨迹。现有方法多只挖掘失败轨迹中仍有进展的片段，却忽视了直接导致任务失败的关键行为。作者认为这两类片段的监督本质上是非对称的：进步片段应被模仿，失败关键片段应被明确规避。

Method: CARF 引入基于进步的重要性评分器，仅在成功专家演示及其扰动结果上训练，用以估计每步对任务完成的贡献，定位失败轨迹中的有效区域。这些评分驱动一个统一的流匹配目标：将策略吸引至进步行为、排斥失败关键行为，同时排除模糊片段，从而避免不可靠监督。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的大量实验中，CARF 在多种失败场景下相较竞争基线取得一致提升；消融实验进一步验证了评分机制与吸引-排斥机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 将失败轨迹中的进步片段与失败关键片段分别作为模仿与规避的非对称监督，能更全面地利用非完美数据，并避免模糊失败片段带来的不可靠监督。项目网站：https://zhao-sq.github.io/carf/#。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is https://zhao-sq.github.io/carf/#.

</details>


### [255] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkelWAM 提出以骨架几何为共享表示的世界-动作模型，实现单源跨形态机器人操作迁移，无需目标任务示范或策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习若要在不同本体间复用操作经验，可显著降低重复的任务专用数据采集成本。但本体变化会同时改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及实现同一工具位姿的全身构型，导致跨形态迁移困难。

Method: 以显式几何表示统一感知与控制：将手臂中心线几何、工具中心点（TCP）位姿与平行夹爪指令构成共享的 25 维状态；该定义同时用于规范化的第三人称与腕部观测以及未来全身动作目标。采用视频-动作混合 Transformer，在预测性视觉监督下训练，预测规范骨架动作块，再由本体特定的约束解码器转换为关节或连续体机器人控制量。该方法不要求关节一一对应。

Result: 在 Franka 上训练的策略于新基准 LIBERO-Cross10（含十项任务、四个形态组下的十种目标本体）上，1,000 个 episode 取得 43.3% 成功率，超出最佳基线 36.2 个百分点；并将 JAKA mini2 训练的策略部署到 Feagine A03 连续体机器人，完成三项桌面操作任务。

Conclusion: SkelWAM 通过单一显式几何表示耦合感知与控制，验证了无需目标任务示范与目标策略更新的单源跨形态迁移可行性，并展示了向真实连续体机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: http://www.liukepku.com/skelwam/index.html

</details>


### [256] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环的机器人密集堆叠流水线，将感知、考虑夹爪的放置优化和力引导执行集成于真实机械臂，实现不规则物体的自动密集装箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库作业中自动密集堆叠需求广泛，但仍是机器人操作的根本挑战。现有不规则物体堆叠工作多针对理想接触的仿真，将物体视为孤立刚体，夹爪往往仅作为事后离散可行性检查，且未处理执行中累积的感知与接触漂移。

Method: 提出包含感知、夹爪感知放置优化与力引导执行的闭环流水线。优化器将物体与夹爪表示为分层球树构成的单一复合体，在GPU上以CMA-ES框架搜索5个自由度，垂直坐标依据当前高度图解析确定。执行时用受力监测的垂直下降在首次接触时停止，释放后通过加固推动消除残留横向间隙，并在每次放置间重新感知容器以避免漂移累积。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda机器人上对3D打印的平面、曲面和凹面物体集合及YCB物体子集进行验证。消融实验分离了夹爪感知优化、加固推动和网格几何对端到端成功率、达成密度和计算成本的贡献。并与高度图最小化方法作为基线进行对比。

Conclusion: 该闭环系统在真实机械臂上有效实现不规则物体的密集堆叠，夹爪感知优化、加固推动与网格几何均对性能有重要贡献。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [257] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究四足机器人运动中步态参数（尤其是占空比）与运动鲁棒性的关系，发现占空比比名义步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛，可作为跨步态与控制架构选择鲁棒运动的低维参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人需在扰动和受限地形中保持稳健运动。现有研究常用步态类型（行走、小跑等）描述运动，但同一步态类型内占空比、速度、站姿宽度等参数仍可变化，因此步态类型无法唯一定义运动。作者希望厘清这些步态参数间的关系。

Method: 采用三种不同的四足运动控制方法进行研究：(1) 全身轨迹优化结合LQR反馈；(2) 基于学习的运动控制器用于占空比选择；(3) 质心模型预测控制框架，并在实体四足机器人上进行窄地形实验验证。

Result: 占空比是比名义步态类型更强的局部误差收敛预测因子；占空比可作为低维参数用于在窄地形环境中调节运动鲁棒性；上述趋势在质心模型预测控制框架下同样成立，并通过实体机器人窄地形实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 占空比为理解和选择鲁棒四足运动提供了一个简单而有效的基础，且这一结论跨步态类型和控制架构均成立。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [258] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIMBO框架，通过在冻结基础控制器周围学习状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF），并将安全结构蒸馏到任务策略中，实现高维全身控制的安全合成，并在29自由度人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身控制需在高度非线性、高维动力学下同时协调避碰与平衡，安全证书难以设计与复用，且在线安全过滤增加了部署负担。

Method: LIMBO从黑盒转移和基于状态的失效规范中学习安全证书（Q-CBF），针对冻结基础控制器周围的残差动作进行合成；学习到的安全值在合成阶段驱动风险引导采样，靠近可恢复性边界；在任务学习阶段作为教师提供动作级安全反馈。

Result: 在29自由度人形机器人上完成躲避球和低障碍下运动任务；风险引导边界采样提供了探索可恢复性边界的理论依据；在相同安全规范下改变采样集中度，可产生从蹲伏到新颖后倾limbo动作的多种策略。

Conclusion: 所学策略无需在线安全过滤即可迁移到硬件，表明学习式安全合成可扩展到敏捷全身控制。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [259] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeeQ通过学习当前活跃子任务的Q值来缩短价值预测视野，无需测试时人工标注即可实现长程操作任务的策略引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂长程任务上表现脆弱，而传统Q值函数因依赖稀疏任务级奖励，面临信用分配视野长、Bellman备份困难和数据覆盖要求高等问题。

Method: 利用离线机器人数据中的子任务级标注提供分解，学习当前活跃子任务的Q值；采用自回归方式用自然语言预测活跃子任务再估计其价值，基于视觉-语言主干，在开源操作数据上预训练并下游微调。

Result: 在两个双臂机器人平台的四个真实世界操作任务上，SeeQ价值函数显著提升了best-of-N策略引导效果。

Conclusion: 基于子任务级Q值的学习能有效缓解长程任务中的信用分配难题，消除测试时对人工标注或模块化子任务预测系统的需求。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RBS-Attention是一种免训练的稀疏预填充方法，通过半径自适应双分支选择解决块质心掩盖高相关token的"均值稀释"问题，在长上下文场景下显著加速推理并保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大模型推理受限于预填充阶段，密集自注意力需在生成前处理整个提示。稀疏块选择可降低成本，但块质心会将高相关token隐藏在大量无关token中，作者称之为"均值稀释"问题。

Method: 提出RBS-Attention免训练稀疏预填充方法，含两个互补选择分支：质心基分支捕捉平均相关性；救援分支利用最大键块半径及其依赖提示、层、头的分布来识别可能被低估的块。两分支独立阈值化并合并掩码，控制救援块贡献，同时保持规则块稀疏FlashAttention执行。

Result: 在H100 GPU上，128K长度下RBS-Attention实现20.65倍独立预填充注意力加速、11.92倍vLLM预填充注意力加速、5.97倍端到端首token时间加速（Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8）。在稠密Qwen3-32B上RULER总体准确率88.65对比稠密注意力的89.52；LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench和Video-MME提供额外质量评估。实验还测量实际保留率、匹配密度下比较选择器，并刻画块大小、阈值和内存行为。

Conclusion: 半径自适应双分支选择是长上下文预填充的有效方法。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [261] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力感知路由（AAR），从注意力权重滑动窗口提取时序与频谱特征来增强MoE路由器的决策，在冻结base transformer的情况下仅训练路由参数，在OLMoE上于GSM8K提升3.37个百分点，并揭示了路由与注意力的耦合回路机制及AAR的深度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-Experts语言模型的router仅依据token的隐藏状态来选择并加权专家，使用的上下文信息有限，忽略了模型中已存在的、已从隐藏状态中解耦的上下文摘要（即注意力状态），因此需要一种能利用更丰富上下文信息的路由机制。

Method: 提出Attention-Aware Routing (AAR)：对注意力权重滑动窗口提取时序与频谱特征，用其增强router，这些特征代表模型上下文状态的摘要并与隐藏状态解耦。保持base transformer完全冻结，仅训练路由参数，将路由作为唯一变量进行隔离研究，并进一步分析路由与注意力的耦合回路及层间应用效果。

Result: 在OLMoE上，AAR相对仅路由SFT基线在GSM8K提升+3.37pp；路由与注意力构成耦合回路——第l层的路由变化经残差流传播，放大第l+1层的注意力汇，从而重塑注意力而无需直接更新注意力机制；AAR减少长且发散的生成，错误答案变短而正确答案长度不变；AAR具有强深度敏感性，普遍应用于各层会损害事实检索，而数学推理的增益在更深层引入时仍能保持。

Conclusion: 层级选择性应用AAR能作为受控探针，揭示不同层注意力所携带的路由相关信息；同时暴露了深度维度上的检索—推理张力，说明路由与注意力紧密耦合，路由设计需考虑层级差异。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [262] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Causal Latent Revision (CaLR)框架，将推理重构为约束隐空间优化，融合自回归模型与扩散语言模型优势，实现并行生成中的动态自纠错，在复杂基准上达到SOTA的DLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型存在局部贪婪问题，而扩散语言模型缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构。作者希望结合两者优点、克服各自缺陷。

Method: 采用来自专家模型的因果拓扑矩阵（CTM）与隐式微分，进行梯度引导的“思维修订”，强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中动态自纠错中间步骤。

Result: 在复杂基准上取得SOTA的DLM性能，超越强自回归基线，并在如数独等约束任务中展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR通过将推理形式化为约束隐优化，有效整合了AR与DLM的优势，实现动态自我纠错，在复杂推理与约束任务中表现优越。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [263] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将DINOv3 ViT以LoRA高效适配到水下SAS自动目标识别，仅单阶段适配即可大幅提升性能，后续堆叠的难负样本挖掘与监督对比学习均无显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径声呐（SAS）自动目标识别对海军能力很重要，但深度学习受限于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波严重以及依赖人工在环评估，因此需要参数高效的适配方法来迁移自然图像预训练模型。

Method: 采用三阶段参数高效框架适配DINOv3 ViT：阶段1冻结ViT主干并引入LoRA，弥合自然图像预训练与水下声学传播差异；阶段2用难负样本挖掘应对岩石、沉积物等声学仿冒物；阶段3用监督对比学习（SupCon）分离目标与杂波表征。在海上SAS数据上以任务级地理划分评估，所有配置在85%测试召回率下比较，并用三个随机种子重复。

Result: LoRA是主要效应来源，在相同冻结主干下将AUPRC从0.300提升到0.679±0.027，Rank 4仅训练0.26%权重即可达到该结果。两个精炼阶段均未超越匹配对照：难负样本挖掘相比同等规模的随机课程AUPRC变化-0.0045±0.0119，SupCon相比前一阶段变化+0.0002±0.0096。

Conclusion: 零结果说明挖掘发生在编码器已拟合的数据上，且监督阶段的目标-杂波几何结构已被基本确定。单个高效适配阶段即足够，堆叠式精炼并无必要。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [264] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析注意力图中的信息流拓扑（基于Forman-Ricci曲率），利用半局部与全局信息流特征来区分大模型中的幻觉与非幻觉回答，并提出一种单次推理的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉检测对于大语言模型（LLM）的可靠部署至关重要。已有基于注意力的方法在区分幻觉与非幻觉回答方面存在不足，因此本文希望从信息流拓扑的全新视角更有效地刻画幻觉特征。

Method: 引入Forman-Ricci曲率来分析注意力图，识别指示信息瓶颈的结构模式；提出一种能同时捕获与幻觉相关的注意力头的半局部与全局信息流特征的方法，实现单次（single-pass）推理。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准上进行了广泛评估，所提出的单次方法与现有基于注意力和多响应基线相比，在两个幻觉检测基准上取得一致的性能提升，并在多种LLM架构上保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 因果生成过程中token间上下文共享受损与幻觉发生密切相关；幻觉回答通常表现为对自注意力的过度依赖、从早期token检索上下文的弥散，或信息过度压缩（over-squashing），尤其在最后一个transformer层中尤为明显。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [265] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究微调如何重塑LLM内部表征，发现EAP识别的任务关键组件集中于特定层（存在功能局部化），但其分布与表征变化最大的层基本无关；跨任务EAP组件重叠并不能带来迁移，任务性质不同时（如分类vs生成）甚至会导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已被广泛用于将LLM适配到各类下游任务，但微调如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解。作者希望通过考察注意力模式与逐层激活的变化，并检验这些变化是否与驱动任务表现的EAP（如注意力头、logit层激活）组件相关，来填补这一空白。

Method: 研究微调对LLM内部表征的影响，分析注意力模式与逐层激活的变化；使用EAP识别驱动任务表现的关键组件（注意力头、logit级激活），并对比这些组件的层级分布与微调中表征变化最大的层；进一步比较不同任务间EAP组件的重叠程度与跨任务迁移表现。

Result: 1）EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，说明模型内化任务特定行为存在一定程度的功能局部化；2）这些组件的层级分布与微调中经历最大表征变化的层基本不相关；3）跨任务的EAP组件重叠在不同性质任务间（如分类vs生成）并不能转化为跨任务性能迁移；4）当两个任务在EAP组件上高度重叠时，在一个任务上微调反而可能损害另一任务的性能。

Conclusion: 微调引入的内部表征变化与EAP所识别的任务关键组件在层级上并不对应；EAP组件的重叠不能保证正向迁移，反而可能带来负迁移。这提示需要更细致地区分"表征变化层"与"任务关键组件层"，并谨慎看待基于组件重叠的迁移假设。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [266] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TinyCeNN-LM质量门控训练后转换框架，将预训练语言模型的注意力层替换为CeNN式细胞循环层，通过验证机制选择性接受转换层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替换预训练语言模型中的注意力机制本质上是一个兼容性问题：看似合理的替代方案可能会改变后续层所期望的表示。因此需要一种保守、可控的转换方式，而非普遍性的注意力替换或追求速度提升。

Method: 提出质量门控训练后转换框架，采用受CeNN启发的细胞循环层，具备有界局部处理、紧凑循环记忆、路由、融合以及接受或回滚验证机制。研究了三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion和PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。严格的PDelta3转换仅在表示和NLL指标均通过固定阈值时才接受该层。

Result: 在SmolLM2-135M上，第0-2层被接受，累计ΔNLL=+0.01209；第3层虽NLL可接受但因表示保真度不达标被拒绝。在Qwen3.5-0.8B上，全注意力层3、7、11被接受，最终ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory将困惑度变化控制在-0.07%至+0.93%之间，同时总缓存减少最多6.01%。对200项下游抽样检查显示，转换后Qwen版本的总体准确率为28.5%-32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，保守的、质量门控的结构转换是可行的，但不应追求普遍性的注意力替换或速度提升。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [267] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个以临床医生为依据的评估平台 InterviewPlayground，基于记忆增强的患者模拟器，用于开放式 AI 精神科问诊的常规质量保证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机构在部署 AI 辅助精神科问诊系统前，需要以临床质量标准对其进行常规评估。由于临床医生问诊风格各异，评估需支持不同问诊方式的可比性、降低临床医生负担，并衡量对部署方具有临床意义的表现。

Method: 构建以临床医生为依据的评估平台 InterviewPlayground，核心是记忆增强的患者模拟器，支持开放式 AI 问诊。使用专家撰写的病例素材创建交互式患者，搭建模拟问诊平台，并设计适用于问诊的评估方式。随后开展 6 名临床医生的 25 分钟试点评估，与基于 GPT 的 LLM 问诊者进行比较。

Result: 在试点中，LLM 问诊者比临床医生恢复了更多嵌入病例中的临床相关条目（88.0% vs. 38.9%），但做出了更多并非基于问诊的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%），且更少对识别出的安全问题进行特征描述（33.3% vs. 66.7%）。

Conclusion: 该平台为 AI 精神科问诊系统的部署质量保证奠定了基础，可在真实医疗场景中用于常规评估与比较。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [268] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AHRR工作流，将Agent-based HLS设计与Post-HLS RTL Refinement结合，在11个任务的基准上相比直接RTL设计实现2.6倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体芯片设计方法大多直接在RTL层面操作，作者希望探索智能体能否通过更高层次抽象（如HLS）设计出更好的芯片。

Method: 对比四种设计流程：Direct RTL Design、Agent-based HLS Design、Post-Compiler HLS Refinement、Post-HLS RTL Refinement，并将Agent-based HLS Design与Post-HLS RTL Refinement组合为AHRR。以FPGA作为端到端评估平台，在11个任务的基准套件上进行测试。

Result: AHRR在该基准套件上相比Direct RTL Design取得2.6倍几何平均加速。案例研究表明HLS将设计知识蒸馏为智能体可利用的抽象，而RTL精炼则恢复了低层次优化机会。

Conclusion: AHRR是面向智能体芯片设计的一个有前景的工作流，代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/ZijD/AHRR.

</details>


### [269] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"特异性优化"(SpecOpt)任务与智能体框架，通过对现有药物进行约束性结构修饰，提升其对目标靶点相对于已知脱靶点的结合偏好，并在915个化合物上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱靶蛋白结合是小分子药物产生不良反应的主要来源，但现有基于结构的分子设计方法多聚焦于从头生成选择性化合物，而非改善已有、表征充分的药物的选择性，这一方向存在明显空白。

Method: 构建基于ChEMBL的基准数据集，通过策展的药物机制注释确定预期靶点、通过实测活性确定脱靶点；开发智能体框架，将化合物与预期靶点及脱靶点对接，通过残基感知的原子-蛋白接触比较对接构象，将差异化相互作用输入大语言模型以提出靶向结构修饰；候选分子仅在满足分子相似性、ADMET及靶点-脱靶点对接选择性标准时才被保留。

Result: 在915个化合物上，该智能体使84.8%的化合物改善了靶点-脱靶点结合差距，平均差距从-0.72 kcal/mol提升至+0.47 kcal/mol，同时与起始化合物的平均Tanimoto相似度保持在0.72。消融实验表明残基特异的接触信息是关键优化信号：将残基身份替换为二元接触指示符后，全部29个消融化合物的改善均消失。

Conclusion: 研究确立了SpecOpt作为一个独立的分子设计问题，并证明残基感知的差异化相互作用是提升现有化合物特异性的有效信号。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>


### [270] [Implicit Rule Induction with Test-Time Task Embeddings in ARC-like Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21181)
*Adrien Deliège,Claas Beger,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两步测试时训练协议 Embed-TTT，先在 VARC 中仅微调任务嵌入再固定嵌入微调骨干网络，从而更好分离规则归纳与规则执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC 类基准难以判断模型成功是真正推断了规则，还是依赖捷径，因此需要更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行。

Method: 在 Vision ARC（VARC）中引入两步测试时训练：第一步仅微调任务嵌入（Embed-TTT），第二步冻结该嵌入并微调骨干网络；并在 ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC 及两个已知规则的受控数据集上评估。

Result: Embed-TTT 持续改善任务嵌入：与底层规则更对齐、提升基于嵌入的检索，并支持对已知规则进行准确线性探测；仅优化少于 0.01% 参数即可解决部分任务，完整两步流程进一步提升性能；还能恢复参数规则的几何结构，实现规则内插但无法外推。

Conclusion: 研究支持在 ARC 类评测中更清晰地区分规则归纳与规则执行，并推动设计能区分分布内与分布外规则的基准。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>


### [271] [AI-GRACE: A Use-Case Operationalization Framework for Agentic AI: From Organizational Objectives and Obligations to Deployment Capabilities and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21192)
*John Cuneo,David Chun,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI-GRACE 框架，将组织治理与技术实现相连接，用于明确代理式 AI 用例中需验证、控制与观测的内容，并以虚构零售银行案例演示其方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署代理式 AI 的组织不能只判断模型是否可信，还需确定某个用例需要验证什么、控制什么、观测什么，才能在满足合规义务的同时实现预期结果。现有治理与技术实现之间存在衔接缺口。

Method: 基于专业观察与标准及文献的目的性综合；采用设计科学界定方法贡献，并用情境方法工程指导情境化裁剪与复用。框架先确立目标与义务，再在七个风险领域（含使命与价值实现）评估风险，进而推导部署前保证、运行时控制与证据需求，并据此开展能力认定、差距评估和逻辑架构设计；通过 Agent Operating Envelope 规定允许行为与升级条件，用 RAIL 概括授权自主程度。

Result: 形成一个可追溯的基础，帮助组织判断必须实现什么、已支持什么、尚未解决什么；并以虚构零售银行应用作为示例说明该方法。

Conclusion: 该贡献为代理式 AI 部署决策提供了可追溯依据，但其是否改善部署决策、效率与复用仍需实证评估。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [272] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoVer 是一个单策略 GRPO 框架，通过信息增益奖励和三阶段多样性选择，解决自博弈代码生成中测试过于宽松和样本集中的问题，在多个基准上提升 pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈方法让单个语言模型同时担任编码者和测试作者，有望突破固定测试套件的限制，但存在两个耦合问题：一是"宽松性崩溃"，即通过无区分度的简单测试即可最大化通过率奖励；二是"集中偏差"，即独立同分布采样的测试集中在常见输入上，导致估计方差增大。

Method: 提出 CoVer，一个单策略 GRPO 框架。第一，设计信息增益（IG）奖励，用自生成测试的通过/失败向量与基于真实答案的分级正确性信号 y∈[0,1]^m 之间的互信息来打分，并以协方差符号作为门控，只奖励具有正区分度的测试。第二，采用三阶段多样性感知选择，通过无效性、输入字符串、执行画像过滤，从候选池中剪枝出行为上非冗余的测试套件，在固定执行预算下提高 IG 估计器的有效样本量。

Result: 在五个基准（LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces）上，CoVer 相比 Qwen2.5-Instruct 主干在 7B 规模上提升 one-shot pass@1 达 +5.8 分，在 14B 规模上提升 +7.1 分，并在两个规模上取得所有对比方法中最高的宏平均。作为 CodeT 排序流程的可插拔主干，CoVer-7B 额外提升 +3.5 分。

Conclusion: CoVer 证明了协同训练对生成和选择均有益处，能同时改善代码生成的训练信号质量与测试选择效果，在多个基准和规模上取得一致提升。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>


### [273] [Ability-Residual Decoupled Modeling for Affective Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21214)
*Boyuan Zhao,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出能力-残差解耦框架用于情感认知诊断，通过显式建模认知残差（学生、题目、概念、学生-概念及低秩学生-题目成分）并让情感模块调节guess/slip，抑制情感表征中的认知污染，在多个数据集与六个认知诊断骨干上取得预测提升与更好的情感对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感认知诊断方法假设认知诊断骨干已解释能力、题目和概念效应，剩余误差可主要归因于情感。但真实教育数据中，题目校准偏差、系统概念偏差、个性化学生-概念偏差及潜在学生-题目匹配会形成稳定的认知残差，若无显式建模路径，这些残差会泄漏进情感表征，导致情感污染（affect contamination）。

Method: 提出能力-残差解耦框架：先通过学生、题目、概念、学生-概念和低秩学生-题目成分捕捉未建模的认知残差；再用情感模块调节猜测/失误（guess/slip）效应；并设计受Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力机制，仅自适应聚合与题目相关的概念残差。

Result: 在ASSIST2017、ASSIST2012、ASSIST2009和Junyi四个数据集、六个认知诊断骨干上，响应预测在报告比较中均有提升；在情感标签可用时情感对齐总体改善。消融实验、泄漏探针、主成分分析可视化、长尾分析和案例研究进一步表明能力残差吸收稳定认知偏差、减少情感分支中的认知污染，并提升鲁棒性与预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模认知残差可有效解耦能力与情感因素，缓解情感污染，提升情感认知诊断模型的鲁棒性和预测准确性。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis infers students' concept mastery from response logs. However, students' responses are not determined by mastery alone: non-cognitive factors such as emotion, engagement, and fatigue can also affect performance. Affective cognitive diagnosis therefore extends conventional cognitive diagnosis by incorporating affective states. Existing methods often assume that the cognitive diagnosis backbone has already explained ability, item, and concept effects, so the remaining errors can be attributed mainly to affect. We argue that this assumption can be insufficient in real educational data: item calibration bias, systematic concept bias, personalized student-concept deviations, and latent student-item matching can form stable cognitive residuals. Without an explicit modeling pathway, these residuals may leak into affective representations, producing affect contamination. To address this problem, we propose an ability-residual decoupled framework for affective cognitive diagnosis. The model first captures unmodeled cognitive residuals through student, item, concept, student-concept, and low-rank student-item components, and then uses an affective module to modulate guess/slip effects. A Q-matrix-constrained concept residual attention mechanism adaptively aggregates only item-relevant concept residuals. Experiments on ASSIST2017, ASSIST2012, ASSIST2009, and Junyi with six cognitive diagnosis backbones show response-prediction gains across the reported comparisons and generally improved affect alignment when affect labels are available. Ablation studies, leakage probes, principal component analysis visualization, long-tail analysis, and case studies further indicate that ability residuals absorb stable cognitive bias, reduce cognitive contamination in the affective branch, and enhance the robustness and predictive accuracy of cognitive diagnosis models.

</details>


### [274] [A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Framework for Perceptual Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21221)
*Hongyan Wei,Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全可微的神经-软符号框架，将视觉感知与任务规划统一在单一计算图中，通过连续软符号状态和可微软转移算子实现端到端优化，在Blocksworld任务上显著优于基线并节省计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知规划任务需同时具备准确感知不确定场景与按逻辑规则规划有效动作序列的能力。传统方法将感知转为离散符号事实后再规划，丢弃了感知不确定性，并切断了任务级反馈对感知的反向作用。

Method: 提出通用全可微的神经-软符号框架，把视觉感知与任务规划连接到单一计算图。维护连续软符号状态，将领域规则提升为可微软T_P转移算子，在短规划视界上优化动作logits；规划目标的梯度可回传更新感知参数，从而在规划过程中精炼任务相关的感知表示。

Result: 在Blocksworld上，本方法解决LatPlan-40的40/40任务、PlanBench-600的596/600任务，优于LatPlan的33/40与推理模型基线的587/600，且计算量和时间大幅减少。感知不确定性消融中，成功率从冻结感知的59%提升到83%。并在Blocksworld场景开展任务与运动仿真，验证解码任务计划与下游机器人运动执行的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了感知与规划的端到端可微耦合，能利用任务级反馈改进感知表示，在准确率、计算效率和执行层面验证上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>


### [275] [CogGym: Towards Large-Scale Comparative Evaluation of Human and Machine Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21259)
*Lance Ying,Jinzhou Wu,Yingshan Susan Wang,Shivam Aarya,Luca M. Schulze Buschoff,Harry Chen,Katherine M. Collins,Andrea de Varda,Shuhao Fu,Sean Dae Houlihan,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang,Samuel Kiegeland,Tetsu Kurumisawa,Rongzhi Liu,Ryan Liu,Ningshan Ma,Kathryn McGregor,Younes Strittmatter,Polina Tsvilodub,Jacob Hoover Vigly,Sarah Wu,Enjie Xu,Yiling Yun,Kelsey Allen,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Brian Christian,Evelina Fedorenko,Michael C. Frank,Michael Franke,Tao Gao,Samuel J. Gershman,Robert D. Hawkins,Jennifer Hu,Julian Jara-Ettinger,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Sydney Levine,Tal Linzen,Hongjing Lu,Timothy O'Donnell,Desmond C. Ong,Steven T. Piantadosi,Rebecca Saxe,Eric Schulz,Tianmin Shu,Felix A. Sosa,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tan Zhi-Xuan,Tomer Ullman,Fei Xu,Ilker Yildirim,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Thomas L. Griffiths,Tobias Gerstenberg,Kevin Smith,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CogGym 框架，用统一标记语言标准化认知科学实验，系统比较大模型与人类行为，发现模型规模越大越接近人类判断，但常识推理差距仍显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 与认知科学都关注人类智能，但人类任务广度大，缺乏可扩展且严谨的人机行为比较方法。

Method: 提出 CogGym，基于认知科学的可扩展统一框架；用半自动、human-in-the-loop 流程将多样实验范式标准化为任务无关的 Experiment Markup Language（EML），实现可复现、忠实的大规模比较。初版整理 100 篇论文中的 258 个认知实验，聚焦人类常识推理，并评估 50 个大语言模型。

Result: 发现明显 scaling 趋势：更大、更新的模型更能复现人类判断。但常识推理任务提升明显慢于数学、编程等形式推理基准；模型与人类拟合仍远低于人类分半信度（人类 R² 为文本 0.93、图像 0.95、视频 0.92；最佳模型仅为文本 0.59、图像 0.58、视频 0.43）。

Conclusion: CogGym 可作为持续更新的活评估框架，纳入新的认知科学实验，刻画模型行为与人类相似和系统性分歧之处，以及随模型与实验演进而变化的模式。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling human intelligence are parallel goals shared by artificial intelligence (AI) and cognitive science. As AI systems grow increasingly capable, in what ways do model responses resemble human responses, and where do they systematically diverge? The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. We introduce CogGym, a scalable, unified framework grounded in cognitive science for systematically comparing model and human behavior on matched experimental trials. CogGym uses a semi-automated, human-in-the-loop pipeline to standardize diverse experimental paradigms into a task-agnostic Experiment Markup Language (EML), enabling reproducible and faithful comparison at scale. For initial release, we curate and standardize 258 cognitive experiments from 100 papers that focuses on human commonsense reasoning, and evaluate 50 large language models against human responses. We find a clear scaling trend where larger and more recent AI models better reproduce human judgments. Yet AI models' improvement on such common reasoning tasks is considerably slower than the gains observed on formal-reasoning benchmarks like math and coding, and model--human fit remains well below human splithalf reliability ($R^2 = 0.93$ on text, $0.95$ on image, and $0.92$ on video) with the best models achieving $R^2 = 0.59$ on text, $0.58$ on image, and $0.43$ on video experiments. We intend for CogGym to provide a living evaluation framework that continually incorporates new cognitive science experiments to characterize where model behavior resembles human behavior, where it systematically diverges, and how those patterns change as models and experiments evolve.

</details>


### [276] [PlaceReasoner-Beta: Reasoning-Driven Macro Placement and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21263)
*Qiufeng Li,Chengxuan Wang,Rongqian Chen,Quan Cheng,Yihui Ren,Chia-Tung Ho,David Z. Pan,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出验证器引导的多智能体框架PlaceReasoner-Beta，将宏单元布局重构为闭环推理问题，并发布开放基准PlaceReasoner-Bench，在布线后PPA上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动宏单元布局是VLSI物理设计的核心难题。现有方法主要优化手工代理目标（如估计线长），且多为一次性数值优化，难以在统一闭环中融合视觉布局上下文、设计经验与下游物理设计反馈。

Method: 提出PlaceReasoner-Beta，将宏布局建模为闭环推理问题：视觉语言模型（VLM）规划器基于布局图像、宏规格与连接结构生成候选布局；几何验证器确保物理合法性与专家布局原则；物理验证器利用早期实现反馈精修候选；布线后优化器基于最终PPA进一步改进。同时构建全开放端到端基准PlaceReasoner-Bench，含8个设计、2种宽高比共16个任务，固定布局与I/O，仅宏位置和朝向可变，以布线后PPA与DRC评估。

Result: 在基准上，PlaceReasoner-Beta在所有正方形任务的DRC洁净方法中取得最佳时序，布线后TNS相比经典基线分别降低61.2%（1:1）和53.0%（2:1）；在多数设计上还缩短了布线线长，尽管从未显式优化线长。

Conclusion: 在物理设计反馈下对空间结构进行推理，可超越代理目标优化，提升端到端布局质量。

Abstract: Automated macro placement remains a fundamental challenge in VLSI physical design. Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [277] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究生产环境分析型LLM智能体的高效重复评测，基于574次历史基准运行（按时间切分），对比随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集和IRT自适应测试。2PL多维自适应测试保真度最佳（200题、38.5%规模、MAE 1.03pp），但实际部署采用难度分层固定子集，因其操作简单且可跨智能体族迁移、跨短校准窗口稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM智能体在不断迭代中需要反复评测，但完整基准重跑成本高昂，缺乏可复用的高效评测方法。作者希望探索如何在保证分数保真度的前提下，降低生产级智能体评测的成本，并结合真实部署经验给出可操作建议。

Method: 使用574次生产基准历史运行数据，按时间顺序划分为校准期与留出期。对比四种评测策略：随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集、基于IRT的自适应测试（多维2PL）。并进一步考察所选方案在五个其他智能体族上的迁移性，以及不同校准窗口长度（最短一天）下的稳定性。

Result: 多维2PL自适应测试总分保真度最高：仅执行200题（占完整运行的38.5%）即可达到1.03个百分点的MAE。实际部署选择了难度分层固定子集，因其操作简便，且无需重新校准即可迁移到五个其他智能体族，并在一日长度的校准窗口下仍然稳定。

Conclusion: 在生产环境中，评测策略的选择需在保真度与运维复杂度之间权衡；虽然自适应测试精度最高，但难度分层固定子集以更低运维成本实现了足够的保真度与良好的可迁移性和稳定性。基于此部署经验，作者提出了面向生产智能体重复评测的实践建议。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience. Using 574 historical runs of the production benchmark, split chronologically into calibration and held-out periods, we compare random sampling, historical caching, fixed representative subsets, and IRT-based adaptive testing. The results show that multidimensional 2PL adaptive testing achieves the best overall score fidelity: executing 200 questions, 38.5% of a full run, yields 1.03 pp of MAE. We nevertheless deployed difficulty-stratified fixed subsets because of their operational simplicity, and show they transfer without recalibration to five other agent families and remain stable across calibration windows as short as one day. Drawing on this deployment experience, we report practical recommendations for recurring production-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [278] [GameASG-Bench: Benchmarking Autonomous Software Generation for Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21293)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Yi Chen,Shusheng Xu,Fan Li,Huan Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GameASG-Bench，将行为可测试性纳入游戏开发生成任务，用静态 L1 与浏览器执行 L2 检查评测 47 个游戏生成任务，揭示高平均通过率掩盖的严格任务成功率（最高仅 55.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件生成虽能把需求转为可执行应用，但交付应用并不等于其交互组件满足指定的行为需求，因此需要把行为可测试性纳入生成任务本身来评测。

Method: 生成前先声明评测接口规范，固定合法起始场景、玩家级动作、稳定快照、拒绝行为与不变量，同时保持私有实现开放；包含静态 L1 源码级检查，以及结合语义观察、真实输入与运行时证据的浏览器执行 L2 检查；实现为 47 个浏览器原生游戏生成任务，覆盖 12 个主要类型及 2D/3D，并配有可执行检查与独立验证的参考实现。

Result: 在九种 agent 组合中，最高平均 L2 检查通过率为 93.2%，但最高严格任务成功率仅 55.3%（26/47）；对 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，完整工具访问与更大标称轮次预算带来更多严格成功，而严格成功率对推理投入并非单调；两种 harness 均各达 18 个严格成功，但仅 10 个任务在两者下都成功。

Conclusion: 高平均检查通过率会掩盖任务级合规缺口，说明需以严格任务成功为准来评估 ASG 系统的行为正确性。

Abstract: Autonomous software generation (ASG) aims to turn human requirements into executable applications, but delivering these applications does not necessarily establish that their interacting components satisfy the specified behavioral requirements. We introduce GameASG-Bench, a benchmark that makes behavioral testability part of the generation task for game development. Our design declares an evaluation interface specification before generation, fixing legal starting scenarios, player-level actions, stable snapshots, rejection behavior, and invariants while leaving private implementations open. Concretely, we include: (i) static L1 checks that assess source-level compliance; and (ii) browser-executed L2 checks that combine semantic observations with real input and runtime evidence. We implement this protocol as 47 browser-native game-generation tasks spanning 12 primary genres and both 2D and 3D interaction, each with executable checks and an independently verified reference implementation. Our experiments answer four key questions about end-to-end agent performance, tool access and nominal turn budget, reasoning effort, and harness choice. Across nine agent stacks, the highest observed mean L2 check pass rate is 93.2%, yet the highest observed strict task success rate, requiring all L1 and applicable L2 prerequisite and core requirement checks, is only 55.3% (26/47 tasks). For DeepSeek-V4-Flash, full tool access and larger nominal turn budgets yield more strict task successes, while the strict task success rate is not monotonic in reasoning effort. Both tested harnesses achieve 18 strict task successes, but only ten tasks succeed under both. These results expose task-level compliance gaps that high average check pass rates actually obscure.

</details>


### [279] [LEGIT: Credentialing Protocol for Trustworthy AI Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21325)
*Steve Drew,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LEGIT——一个连接认证、声誉与市场分配的凭证协议，用于解决代理市场中买家难以判断哪个AI代理表现最佳的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI代理市场中，买家难以确定哪个代理在其任务上表现最佳；已报告的基准分数难以验证，且难以跨任务、软件和预算进行比较。

Method: LEGIT凭证协议：认证将测得的每个已解决任务的质量与成本，通过签名记录绑定到代理配置、任务域、评估预算和证据上；声誉将过往任务结果记录与同一身份关联，并受反馈报告可靠性的约束；买卖双方可验证凭证记录并查看可选的可视化档案。

Result: 评估揭示了在观察到的任务成功率相近的代理配置之间存在成本差异，并表明比较结果依赖于评估预算；补充分析在给定的女巫攻击模型下量化了操纵声誉所需的押金和费用。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持将性能测量绑定到所测试的配置和资源限制上，从而提升代理市场的可信度与可比性。

Abstract: Agentic marketplaces are emerging where AI agents with varying capabilities autonomously complete specialized tasks for buyers. A major challenge of such marketplaces is that buyers cannot easily determine which agent will perform best on their tasks. Reported benchmark scores may be difficult to verify or compare across tasks, software, and budgets. We introduce LEGIT, a credentialing protocol connecting certification, reputation, and proposed marketplace allocation. Certification binds measured quality and cost per solved task to an agent configuration, task domain, evaluation budget, and evidence through a signed record. Reputation links records of past task outcomes to the same identity, subject to the reliability of the reported feedback. Buyers and agents can verify credential records and inspect optional visual profiles. Evaluations reveal cost differences between agent configurations with similar observed task success, and show that comparisons depend on the evaluation budget. These results support binding performance measurements to the tested configuration and resource limits. A complementary analysis quantifies the deposits and fees required for reputation manipulation under a stated Sybil attack model.

</details>


### [280] [Offline Multimodal Large Language Models for Decision Support in Air Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21390)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Jelton A. Cunha,Gabriel Dietzsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在无网络、受限安全环境下部署离线大语言模型作为决策支持工具，提出一种模块化检索增强架构，支持文本与图像输入，并通过巴西空军图像分析员试点研究验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中作战依赖复杂规则、既定程序和时效性强的分析，而分析员常在连接受限、安全约束严格的环境中工作，需将书面条令与图像结合，却往往无法访问外部计算资源，因此需要可离线、可溯源的知识支持工具。

Method: 提出一种适用于无互联网连接的模块化检索增强（RAG）架构，支持来自技术手册的文本和图像输入，通过自然语言交互提供可追溯至原始来源的条令知识；开展含四名巴西空军图像分析员的试点研究，包括（i）基于电子目标识别条令的知识评估，比较人类与系统在同一测试中的表现；（ii）测量无AI辅助手工编写侦察目标报告（REMIR）的认知负荷。

Result: 手工任务负荷较高，心理需求为6.0/7，努力程度为5.0/7；所提系统得分与人类持平（8/10），完成评估仅需7.1分钟，而人类平均需26.5分钟，为未来AI辅助评估建立了基线。

Conclusion: 离线大语言模型结合检索增强架构可作为受限环境下的可行决策支持工具，在保持表现的同时显著提升效率；作者最后提出未来评估协议，以系统比较手工与AI辅助工作流程。

Abstract: Air operations rely on complex rules, established procedures, and time-critical analysis under limited connectivity and strict security constraints. In such environments, analysts must combine written doctrine with images, often without access to external computing resources. This paper studies offline large language models as decision support tools, deployed in isolated and restricted environments to give analysts access to doctrinal knowledge that remains traceable to its original sources through natural language interaction. We describe a modular retrieval-augmented architecture suitable for operation without Internet connectivity, supporting both text and image input from technical manuals. As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we report a pilot study with four image analysts of the Brazilian Air Force, combining (i) a doctrinal knowledge assessment based on their electronic-target identification doctrine, comparing human and proposed system performance on the same test, and (ii) a measurement of the cognitive workload involved in manually producing a reconnaissance target report (Relatório de Missão de Reconhecimento - REMIR) without AI assistance. The results show a demanding manual task, especially in terms of mental demand (6.0/7) and effort (5.0/7), while the proposed system matches the human score (8/10) and completes the assessment in 7.1 minutes (compared to a human average of 26.5 minutes), establishing a baseline for future AI-assisted evaluation. Finally, we describe a future evaluation protocol to systematically compare manual and AI-assisted workflows.

</details>


### [281] [DENSE: Distilling Agent Trajectories into Evidence-Grounded Shortcut Trees for Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21423)
*Siyuan Liu,Fan Yu,Dongyu Ru,Yizhu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DENSE 方法，将智能体执行轨迹蒸馏为可复用的证据化反馈，并设计 REFIT 评测协议，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上取得最高严格通过率并减少重跑 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体部署会产生大量执行轨迹，但任务特定验证与专家标注成本高昂、难以规模化；因此需要一种无需事后结果标签、即可将轨迹蒸馏为可复用反馈的方法。

Method: DENSE 将轨迹证据组织为"证据锚定的嵌套捷径树"，压缩冗余尝试、利用恢复证据协调跨层级问题，对已完成分支进行摘要、对未解决分支继续展开，从而把可复用进展与剩余义务关联。同时提出 REFIT 源配对协议，在事后结果盲条件下比较同一初始轨迹的反馈，并重置环境与模型上下文进行同任务重试。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，DENSE 在四种受体模型中取得所测非特权反馈方法中最高的严格通过率；相比初始执行，严格通过率提升 7.12–15.64 个百分点，重跑时观测到的受体 token 减少 19.0–43.6%。GPT-5.5 消融实验支持将嵌套子任务分析与捷径构建、问题协调相结合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于证据的轨迹复用可使智能体实现自我改进，并减少对外部监督的依赖。

Abstract: Online agent deployments produce abundant execution traces, while task-specific verification and expert annotation are costly to scale. We study how to distill these traces into reusable feedback without post-hoc outcome labels, drawing on their evidence of local progress, recovery, and unfinished requirements. We introduce DENSE (Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions), which organizes this evidence into evidence-grounded nested shortcut trees. DENSE compresses redundant attempts, reconciles issues across levels using recovery evidence, and summarizes completed branches while expanding unresolved ones, linking reusable progress to remaining obligations. We introduce REFIT, a source-paired protocol comparing feedback from shared initial trajectories under post-hoc outcome blindness, with environments and model contexts reset for fresh attempts at the same tasks. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, DENSE achieves the highest strict pass rate among tested non-privileged feedback methods across four recipient models. Relative to initial executions, strict pass rate improves by 7.12-15.64 pp, with 19.0-43.6% fewer observed recipient tokens in reruns. GPT-5.5 ablations support combining nested subtask analysis with shortcut construction and issue reconciliation. These findings point toward agent self-refinement through evidence-grounded trajectory reuse with less reliance on external supervision.

</details>


### [282] [GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21432)
*Kaichen Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dingqian Hong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GVPO（组方差策略优化），一种新的LLM后训练方法，通过将KL约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权方案，消除了对重要性采样的依赖，保证唯一最优解，并可自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏（OPD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如GRPO）依赖重要性采样，导致训练不稳定，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 提出GVPO，将KL约束奖励最大化的解析解整合进梯度加权方案。其梯度对应于隐式奖励中心距离与实际奖励中心距离之间的均方误差；无需重要性采样，可采用灵活采样分布。

Result: GVPO保证唯一最优解，且恰好对应KL约束奖励最大化目标；支持灵活采样分布而无需重要性采样；并自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏（OPD），可优化一族扩展的OPD目标。

Conclusion: GVPO统一了理论保证与实际适应性，为可靠、通用的LLM后训练与在线策略蒸馏建立了新范式。

Abstract: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling.
  We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling.
